## Hyderabad

*oṁ ajṅāna-timirāndhasya jṅanaṅjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.**

Our spiritual master gave us knowledge that was not to be found in books of Sanskrit grammar, nor was it to be realized in meditation on the seashore, but the knowledge which purifies our consciousness was given in the form of words emanating from his lotus mouth. Just as there exist rivers of nectar flowing on the moon planet, so there is a constant flow of nectar from the lotus lips of our Gurudeva. This nectar is now immortalized in the form of “the books of His Divine Grace.” We may be reading your books, printing your books, or distributing your books, but we must live by your books, for we know that by your books you are with us and are helping us to fight off the illusory energy.

Knowledge of Kṛṣṇa, freedom from *māyā*, is such a great gift that it is impossible to repay the benefactor. Pṛthu Mahārāja requested the Kumāras to be satisfied by their own benevolent activities. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we must humbly make a similar request to you. A joker’s activities simply around laughter, and anyone who tries to repay the spiritual master becomes a laughingstock just like a joker, because it is not possible to repay such a debt.

*Your eternal debtors of ISKCON, Hyderabad.*

## Kathmandu

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

How is it possible to estimate our good fortune to be disciples of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda? Only by great fortune are we attached to his lotus feet and so are attached to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Such attachment is the perfection of our lives. The nature of our *guru* is described in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* as follows: “According to the deliberate opinion of all the revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa, in the form of the spiritual master, delivers His disciples.”

The International Society for Kṛṣṇa consciousness is a divine form of Śrīla Prabhupāda in which we have our life and being. Śrīla Prabhupāda’s desires give us the impetus to serve Kṛṣṇa by spreading *kṛṣṇa-kathā* in every country of the world. His mercy comes as many instructions for our advancement in spiritual life. Everything we possess, including our mind and body, is his property, and we are thankful to him for maintaining our worthless lives. Already Prabhupāda has made his plan and shown us by his example how to act, and for our part we can follow him in our own small ways. Prabhupāda’s words are the potency we have to change the hearts of men, and by the distribution of his words we give him great pleasure. Just like sold-out animals, we find our security and protection only in his service.

Just as a thief sees with great expectation the drowsiness of the watchman and prepares to sneak and take his due, in the same way the witch called Māyā is waiting for that single moment when we lose our tight grip – just a little – on his lotus feet. At that very time of deviation, there and then, we shall find ourselves fallen in her kingdom again. Here in the kingdom of Nepal, we disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda pray that we shall never lose our hold of Prabhupāda’s lotus feet and that we can always relish the association of our Godbrothers as we try and do his divine will. We pray that we can always hear, with rapt attention, the presence of Prabhupāda in his divine books. O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please be especially merciful to us!!

Mahā-Viṣṇu dāsa brahmacārī / Gauracandra dāsa brahmacārī / Tīrtharāja dāsa brahmacārī / Durvāsā dāsa brahmacārī / Pītāmbara dāsa brahmacārī / Dhurvanātha dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Hans / Bhakta Basanta

## Laguna Beach

We remember Your Divine Grace as the perfect *ācārya.* By your words and actions, you taught us everything – how to live, how to work, how to eat, how to be clean, how to raise our children, how to surrender to Kṛṣṇa, how to be happy, and finally, how to die. You became our savior, our life and soul. The few times that we were blessed with your *darśana* are pure, sacred memories which we will always hold deep within our hearts. Although you have left this physical universe, you cannot leave our hearts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your causeless mercy you appeared and spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. By translating *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Bhagavad-gītā, Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and many other volumes, and by establishing the chanting of the holy names, Deity worship, and book publication and distribution, you have shown us the real goal of life and have given us the means to attain it. And in leaving, you gave us the greatest gift, our “burden of love”: to carry on your missionary work of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

By working to fulfill your mission, we are being protected from *māyā’s* infection, and conditioned souls continue to receive the mercy of Kṛṣṇa. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the cloud of mercy, and your life-giving rains will continue to fall on the parched victims of the fire of material existence. We simply want to go on witnessing the showering torrents of your mercy as we distribute your books everywhere. Please empower us so that we can distribute more and more.

Every day we meet hundreds of conditioned souls who are working hard just to find a little pleasure in this material world. Just as the conditioned souls are acting under material natures’ stringent laws, we (your disciples) are also acting – but under the shelter of your transcendental order: “Go and make the unfortunate souls fortunate.”

By studying your books and by working to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we are able to realize the futile misery of trying to enjoy this material world. You have taught us to perceive the miseries of materialistic life, and thus you have made it impossible for us to return to that illusion. You have saved us, and you are continuing to save us by your unflinching mercy.

Because we are feeling the soothing shelter of your lotus feet, because we have a glimpse of your fervent desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world, because we are realizing our eternal debt to you, and because we love you, we will not deviate in carrying out your order. We will not fall down and disappoint you, or concoct anything new – because we are constrained by love.

Of course, we are always feeling the temptations of material desires, but by remembering your words our minds become peaceful. You have given us too much mercy, so much service. How could we loiter any longer in this material world? Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are simply amazed by your causeless mercy, as we feel ourselves being *dragged* back to Godhead. You have made us so ecstatic that we jubilantly chant and dance and want only to serve your mission of inundating the universe with such sweet mercy.

Now we are remembering your last and most urgent request: that we continue with your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and not quarrel and fight among ourselves. Admittedly, we run up against disagreements and personal likes and dislikes. (Actually, the entire material world is in turmoil over petty disputes.) But as your disciples, we will not allow our difference to weaken us. We understand that ISKCON is *your* work. We are only your obedient servants, united by the desire to carry on *your* mission …cooperatively.

## La Paz

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda:

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

How can we ever express our thankfulness for your taking us out of this dark well of ignorance? We are constantly praying to Śrī Kṛṣṇa to give us a clear mind to distinguish where is Kṛṣṇa and where is *māyā* and to maintain us firmly serving eternally your lotus feet, distributing your books and the holy names of the Lord to all living entities. Please give us your mercy. We are eagerly seeking refuge in the shadow of your lotus feet.

*Your unworthy servants at La Paz Mandira.*

## Lima

Do you think he has gone? Do you think he has become unconscious of his is? Śrīla Prabhupāda once said in Caracas, Venezuela: “I am both externally and internally conscious of everything that happens within the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement.”

He was conscious then; he is conscious now. This consciousness has not ceased to exist, nor can it be limited (Bg. 2.12). Being an absolute personality, and having concern with those who love him, there can never exist a separation. “Love” means connection.

Just as Śrī Kṛṣṇa, by His supreme will, can leave behind as remnants His spiritual paraphernalia, so also this extremely powerful spiritual personality, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to increase the love of his disciples, has left the body which he has used to execute his pastimes here in this material world. Of course, Śrīla Prabhupāda could have left himself in the same body, but out of his kindness he has left his remnants so that we fallen souls can always glorify and remember him.

Do you miss him? Hear his words and he is there. Follow his instructions, execute his order, and you will always attract his presence. The absolute spiritual entities are not limited by the material energy. We must think, “If I do not make your order my life, then I will never gain your association.” If you do not want to see him, to feel his presence, to be with him, then forget what he has told you, forget what he has said. But if you want that cherished association, if you want to be with him now, then do what he wanted, execute his will, go out and preach this Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, and as sure as the sun rises and sets, you will one day be with him face to face in Vaikuṇṭha, serving him as you have done even in this life. He is our worshipable deity, our living force, savior, and divine inspiration. We take shelter of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

## London (Bhaktivedanta Manor)

In the desert of material life  
Doomed forever to remain  
Our shriveled hearts lay parched within  
Without your mercy rain.

But then throughout these desiccated depths  
That clement shower did fall  
Inundating everything  
Revitalizing all.

Gasping souls were given life  
Their miseries washed abroad  
Sinful aspirations all but drowned  
In that redeeming flood.

Being recipients of that rain we caught  
Some drops fresh like dew  
And pray they cleanse these envious hearts  
So that we may glorify you.

Impossible the task to describe  
A heart that has no measure  
Mere words alone cannot contain  
That supreme spiritual treasure.

At once majestic and unproud  
Regal bearing without flaw  
With gentleness you guide all common men  
True champion of the poor.

God’s greatest glory divine love  
Incarnate in your form  
Your benign grace this earth pervades  
But was in heaven born.

And in that purest holy mind  
No selfish though would keep  
You would only think of suffering man  
And thinking of him, weep.

To mitigate the miseries of mankind  
You worked both day and night  
You kindled the fire to light the world  
And gave blind men their sight.

With mankind’s future tenderly cradled  
In your loving hands  
Your teachings show the way back home  
To people of all lands.

Your books a sense of wonder leave  
A revolution they have begun  
A monument to Your Divine Grace  
For all times to come.

And in those distant far-off days  
Your glories shall endure  
In the annals of history indelibly inscribed  
To remain for evermore.

And the historians of eternity will tell and retell  
Of your great mercy-errand to deliver souls from hell  
And the pious people of that future generation  
Will listen with wonder and great veneration.

And throughout the ages just as now  
They’ll receive your matchless gift  
Your immortal words the chance to serve  
The greatest man who lived.

Impossible for us to appreciate  
The sacrifice you have made  
We’ll lay our lives at your feet  
But you’ll never be repaid.

O Prabhupāda, give us the heart  
To carry on your mission  
Your mercy is all that gives us life  
Your perfect task, our vision.

Our fervent prayers, O lord, to you  
Though trials we may meet  
Allow us forever to remain  
Serving your lotus feet.

*Your eternal servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor.*

## London (Bury Place)

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet.

This is the first time that we are writing a Vyāsa-pūjā offering while you are no longer physically present on this planet. Your departure, causing intense feelings of separation in the hearts of your disciples, is a completely new test for us. A real test of our faith in the *guru*, a real test by which our realization is examined, of how much we are aware of the fact that the relationship between you and your disciples is a purely transcendental arrangement, and that this relationship cannot be checked by the laws of material nature. Maybe we have had friends or relatives, uncles or parents pass away, and we can probably recall the strange but mundane atmosphere surrounding such events. Everything appeared false and like a dream, without any real substance. We might even have felt something like separation, but of course completely based on the concept of “Oh, I am so sad he is gone, because now he cannot give me any more sense gratification.” But there is absolutely no comparison with the feelings we experienced when you left this mortal plane. I think that this is the test that reveals that somehow or other by your causeless mercy we have develop attachment for you, in spite of the fact that we had no taste for spiritual life. Somehow or other, by your causeless mercy we have made some advancement on the path of devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. That also reveals that your personality was so attractive that we became attached to you, in spite of our deep-rooted distaste for the uncompromising spiritual instructions emanating from your lotus lips. And the most important aspect of all these revelations is that strange but pleasant and reassuring feeling that you never left us, showing that our attachment to you is not mundane. It is not like one of those ordinary and temporary relationships in the material world, but it is an eternal, spiritual relationship of a superexcellent and inconceivable nature, although we are not fully aware of it. But a glimpse of that awareness is manifesting itself, giving us new strength, knowing that you are guiding and watching us at every moment through the agent of Kṛṣṇa’s inconceivable potencies. Of course, we already knew this from the revealed scriptures, but this direct confrontation has added a new dimension to our understanding.

But the ultimate test is whether we will be able to maintain this understanding and expand it in the future, since you are no longer here personally to encourage or chastise us, or as you yourself once stated in a letter, “to keep whipping us.” And this will be shown in the way we carry on your mission. Our real faith in you will be shown in the way we are willing to sacrifice our own lives to fulfill your mission, just as you sacrificed everything to satisfy your spiritual master. But the greatest sacrifice is to always remain humble and meek, since the instruction is that one should feel like a fool in from of his *guru*. We should never forget that you are always here with us. To feel oneself humble and insignificant means that no matter what position we occupy, we never think, “I can do better than my *guru*. He was too compassionate,” or “He did this wrong or that wrong.” Abstaining from such ill thoughts, we may then also become *gurus,* since this was always your desire, that every one of us should become an *ācārya*. And one of your most outstanding qualities was your unlimited compassion and love for all living beings, no matter how fallen or degraded they were. This was clearly shown in the way you picked us up out of the filthy gutters of *māyā*. So we should humbly follow in your footsteps, endeavoring to develop the same qualities.

We have caught a glimpse of attachment to your lotus feet. Now we must be careful not to neglect this glimpse or to forget it in due course of time. We must deepen this feeling towards you at every moment. Then nothing can go wrong, and even the strongest temptation will not be able to stand in front of such genuine feelings of affection. Then we will be successful in glorifying you and the great mission which you have started. Then we will be able to fully establish that God conscious society the way you were envisioning it. And finally, by your causeless mercy, so many people will become pure devotees of the Lord and attain the perfection of life. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an ocean of compassion, and we simply pray that we may never forget your lotus feet and that we may never forget serving them.

Your most fallen servants from Śrī Śrī Rādhā-London-īśvara Dhāma

## Los Angeles

When Pṛthu Mahārāja met the four Kumāras, he stated that “great personalities can be satisfied only by their own activities, which are distributed amongst human society out of their unlimited mercy.” Therefore, our attempt to glorify you can best be done by describing your activities, otherwise we can see no other means of satisfying you. We saw a living example of this last year in London on Vyāsa-pūjā day, the very last Vyāsa-pūjā day we would be able to spend with you in your manifest presence. As you sat in your elevated palanquin across from Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda, your eyes gazing on them with love beyond our comprehension, your servant began to recite the history of Your Divine Grace’s transcendental activities in this material world. We gazed up at Your Divine Grace in your weakened state, and we watched as you tilted your head back, and your face spread with a glowing smile of eternal satisfaction. In this wonderful mood of ecstasy, you sat and heard your pastimes, the activities of one who is always in direct communication with Kṛṣṇa. May we always be immersed in remembrance of this scene on that final Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Now, one year later, we are feeling the pangs of separation from your lotus feet. The Vyāsa-pūjā book is complete, but for the first time we are unable to mail you the first copies from the printer. In this condition, we can remember the glorious times when we would bring you a new book, the tangible result of your hours of selfless labor, and these memories bring us back once again into the peaceful, loving shelter of your association. Please allow us to recall some of these incidents for our own benefit and your eternal glorification.

One time we brought you a new *Back to Godhead* magazine. On the back cover was an advertisement showing a stack of your books extending up into the sky. With a wonderful smile, you looked at the tower of books and said, “Come on!” challenging the entire world to try to defeat you. A *mahā-ratha* is nothing compared to you. He can only fight with ten thousand warriors at a time, while you are prepared to battle with all the demoniac living entities within this material world. Thus your father named you after the “fearless lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa.”

The last time you were in your beloved Māyāpur, you were looking at some new paintings, although your sickness had you in a very weakened state. You came to the picture of Lord Rāmacandra breaking the bow of Lord Śiva at the assembly where mother Sītā was to choose her husband, with all the competitors falling down unconscious. Looking up at us, you smiled and said in a feeble voice, “It is not so easy business to marry Sītādevī.” When an ordinary man would have been in great distress, you continued to inspire us with your transcendental sense of humor in your relationship with the Lord.

On another occasion, in New York at the time of the Fifth Avenue Ratha-yātrā, your last days ever in America, a new *Bhāgavatam* volume was ready at the printer. In our own foolish way, we decided we would surprise you. We picked up the books at the airport and arrived back at the temple well past midnight. As usual, you were awake, working on your translations. Your slippers rested outside the door to your *darśana* room, while you were around the corner and down the hall by your bedroom. Being your transcendental children, we hid the new book behind our backs and waited for you to come around the corner. As you rounded the corner, you saw us standing there with huge grins and our hands behind our backs. Chuckling, you walked into your *darśana* room, motioned with your hand, and said, “Come on!” (your all-purpose slogan). As you stood on your *āsana* preparing to sit, we were unable to control ourselves any longer and handed you the beautiful new gold-bound book. In great ecstasy, you touched the book to your head and began to page through it, so immersed that you didn’t even sit down. As always, you spoke the introductory verse and sat down to look at the paintings. Then, deep in thought, you paged through the book and read to us and preached for an hour and a half. It was impossible to appreciate your transcendental glories. Without caring for your body, you could go on forever talking about Kṛṣṇa and His devotional service. You knew no limits to the pleasure of *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanam* because you are an intimate associate of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa who descended on this world at Their request to reclaim Their fallen servants.

Feeling the pangs of separation while remembering these transcendental activities of Your Divine Grace, we can only follow in the footsteps of Arjuna when he heard of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s disappearance from the earth:

“Due to grief, Arjuna’s mouth and lotuslike heart had become dried up. Therefore his body lost all luster. Now, remembering the Supreme Lord, he could hardly utter a word in reply. With great difficulty he checked the tears of grief that smeared his eyes. He was very distressed because Lord Kṛṣṇa was out of his sight, and he increasingly felt affection for Him. Remembering Lord Kṛṣṇa and His well wishes, benefactions, intimate familial relations and His chariot driving, Arjuna, overwhelmed and breathing very heavily, began to speak. Arjuna said: ‘O King! The Supreme Personality of Godhead Hari, who treated me exactly like an intimate friend, has left me alone. Thus my astounding power, which astonished even the demigods, is no longer with me. I have just lost Him whose separation for a moment would render all the universes favorable and void, as bodies without life.’”

After relating some of his pastimes with the Lord, Arjuna finally stated, “Now I am attracted to those instructions imparted to me by the Personality of Godhead \[Govinda\] because they are impregnated with instructions for relieving the burning heart in all circumstances of time and space. Sūta Gosvāmī said, “Thus being deeply absorbed in thinking of the instructions of the Lord, which were imparted in the great intimacy of friendship, and in thinking of His lotus feet, Arjuna’s mind became pacified and free from all material contamination.”

So, in the same way that Arjuna’s distress could only be relieved by Kṛṣṇa’s instructions, we can once again live in your association by following your instructions. You have told us many times that everything is in your books; there is no need to look anywhere else for shelter. Anybody that reads your books knows what to do – chant sixteen rounds, follow the four regulative principles, attend the temple programs, preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness vigorously and serve the Lord with every spare moment, under the direction of the *guru.* If we can simply do these things, you have guaranteed us that we will return back home, back to Godhead. We therefore humbly pray at your lotus feet to give us the strength to constantly remember and follow your instructions. We lie prostrate at your feet and beg this benediction from you. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please pray to your Guru Mahārāja and self-realization Śrī Gaura-Nitāi to empower all of your disciples to always follow your instructions. If we can accept this mercy, then it is guaranteed that this movement will overwhelm the world, and those who sincerely participate will one day again have the opportunity of sitting at your lotus feet, receiving your merciful glances of love.

Los Angeles

## Manipur

*oṁ ajṅāna-timirāndhasya jṅanaṅjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Your Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, the king of all the Vaiṣṇavas, lord and master of all the devotees, often expressed that, “I want to make Manipur, land of Babhruvāhana the son of Arjuna, the first scientific Vaiṣṇava state of ISKCON.” Everyone in Manipur was ready to receive your divine lotus feet ever since the name of ISKCON became known. Most Vaiṣṇavas in Manipur have expressed that it is their greatest misfortune that your planned visit to Manipur did not occur. However, because of your desire, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, made everything happen.

ISKCON Manipur was opened on December 25, 1977, according to the directions that Your Divine Grace mercifully gave us just before your departure from this physical world. We simply acted as your mailmen and delivered whatever Your Divine Grace lovingly instructed us to. The coming of ISKCON to Manipur was received with great expectation by the true Vaiṣṇavas, and the first Hare Kṛṣṇa Festival of Manipur was held on April 7, 8, and 9. Many scholars and leaders in Manipur participated in this festival with great enthusiasm. Our ISKCON *ārati* is becoming the “talk of the town” in Manipur. Every day at least 150 people are coming for the evening *Bhagavad-gītā* class, and they greatly appreciate our ISKCON activities.

The First International Bhāgavata-dharma Discourse in the history of Manipur was held on April 21. Devotees from the U.S.A., Brazil and Italy participated in this historic event. All of the inhabitants of Manipur were simply amazed to see our Western Godbrothers becoming purified Vaiṣṇavas. This is the priceless gift of Your Divine Grace.

So, everything is beginning to happen in Manipur as Your Divine Grace envisioned. We beg to be eternally guided by your divine instructions. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your eternal servants,*

Lokanātha Svāmī / Bhakti-cāru Svāmī / Svarūpa Dāmodara dāsa brahmacārī / Mahāmanā dāsa brahmacārī / Gurudeva dāsa brahmacārī / Manu dāsa brahmacārī / Cintāmaṇi dāsa brahmacārī / Vanamālī dāsa brahmacārī / Puruṣottama dāsa brahmacārī / Digpāla dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Rādhā-mohana / Bhakta Ibotomba / Bhakta Jīvana

## Maracaibo

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Every day we are all experiencing more and more of your separation, and we feel a pain that increases our love for you. You are the lord and master of all devotees, and therefore our hearts feel real rejoicing when we meditate on taking shelter at your lotus feet. Your Divine Grace has given everything in the form of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your merciful associate is matchless, because just through it we can attain pure love of Kṛṣṇa. We wish to develop our personal pure relationship with you, and thus feel completely confident, with no fear at all, being protected by the shadow of your lotus feet. Our perfection is to fulfill your desires, which are implicit in your books, in your words, in your example, and in all the transcendental purposes you started. Therefore, Your Divine Grace is the fountainhead of our inspiration for loving Kṛṣṇa purely, and there can never be anything more magnanimous or significant in our lives than Your Divine Grace, who opened our eyes with the torchlight of transcendental knowledge.

In the innermost region of our feelings there is recorded the sweet and spiritual sound and emanation from your lotus lips, the dazzling effulgence that emanates from your deity, and the tender and magnificent character of your spotless pure devotion for the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

O Gurudeva, *patita-pāvana*! After transmigrating over and over this ocean of miseries, you have planted in our hearts the seed of *bhakti*. Taking shelter at your lotus feet in this homage to Your Divine Grace, we are all prepared to serve your eternally and fulfill your instructions with the best of our faith and devotion, which increases day after day, although we are so fallen.

*yasya prasādad bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“By the mercy of the spiritual master one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. Therefore, I should always remember and praise the spiritual master. At least three times a day I should offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master.”**

Please engage us eternally in your sublime devotional service. All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Your most fallen servants, from the *harer nāma mahā-mantra’s* Maracaibo Mandira.

## Mauritius

**Prabhupāda’s Mercy**

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu declared,  
Let no one on the earth be spared,  
To every corner you must go,  
The holy name they all must know.

As a glimpse of the morning sun  
Drives darkness away from one,  
So the holy sound from lips divine  
Can cause a heart steel-framed to shine.

But this world is a dangerous place,  
And personal ambition can mean disgrace,  
For many years the mission slowed,  
The earth was deep in terror bowed.

Many great stalwarts easily fell  
To jackals and snakes within a dry well,  
Allured by palaces built on the air  
Or bewildered by ghosts, all was despair.

There was need of a great and powerful soul,  
In the mold of Prahlāda and Nārada of old,  
A perfect devotee, the time was now due  
For His Divine Grace, the truth to imbue.

The wisdom of Kṛṣṇa’s eternal line,  
Not theories and gestures changing with time,  
The nectarean songs of Bhaktivinoda  
Replaced the prattle of popular ode.

As Bhaṭṭahārīs stole the Lord’s Kṛṣṇadāsa  
We’d been polluted and rendered fifth class,  
You appeared shining like the Lord Gaurāṅga,  
We were free! Our captors torn asunder.

O moonlike devotee of eternal fame,  
I pray for the world to remember your name,  
For this alone can give them relief,  
And the seed of devotion at His lotus feet.

(Verse 7, line 1, refers to *Madhya-līlā,* Vol. 4, pages 31-34; verse 4, lines 2-4, refer to *Bhāg.*, Canto 5, “The Forest of Enjoyment.”)

Suniketana dāsa / Kapila dāsa / Somendranātha dāsa

## Mayapur Dhama

*1. prabho prabhūnāṁ he guro gurūṇāṁ*  
*prabhu-kṛpā-mūrti-kaly-avatīrṇaḥ*  
*na tava kriyāḥ prabhu-śakti-hīnās*  
*tat prabhupāda iti nāma-yuktam*

**“O lord of lords, master of spiritual masters, the mercy incarnation of the Supreme Lord appearing in the age of Kali, what you have done is not devoid of the empowering energy of the Lord. Therefore to call you ‘Prabhupāda’ is fitting and proper.”**

*2. jagad-gurur bhūtvā labdha-pratiṣṭho*  
*nānyatra gacchaty anyaḥ sādhu-veśī*  
*jagad-uddhārāt tu tvad-viśva-khyāter*  
*jagad-gurur ity upādhir hi sādhuḥ*

**“Becoming ‘spiritual masters of the universe’ and thus getting great prestige, others, dressed as** ***sādhus*** **do not go to any other place. But because you have delivered the whole universe and are famed all over the world, therefore, to call you** ***jagad-guru*** **is most correct.”**

*3. gauḍīya-vādasya kāruṇya-sāraṁ*  
*prāyo ‘pi nṣṭaṁ kali-kāla-yogāt*  
*pracārayan tat punar jīvayaṇ ca*  
*jagat samagraṁ prabhupāda trāsi*

**“The essence of the message of the Gauḍīya-sampradāya is mercy upon the conditioned souls. This was practically lost due to the influence of the age of Kali. You have preached and thereby given the essence new life. And thus, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have delivered the whole universe.”**

*4. audārya-līlānubhāvāya yasmai*  
*rāmādi-guru-svarūpāya sākṣāt*  
*prabhupādādāya śrī-guru-varāya*  
*namaḥ samprādāya-pālāya tubhyaṁ*

**“You are the only one who understood the munificent pastimes of Lord Caitanya. Therefore you are the direct manifestation of the original** ***guru*****, Nityānanda Rāma. O Prabhupāda, the best of spiritual masters, I offer my obeisances to you, the preserver of the disciplic succession.”**

*5. mahāprabhor guṇa-gānāmṛtaṁ tu*  
*śrī-bhāgavataṁ tvayānuditaṁ ca*  
*mleccha-bhāṣāyām atak kim āśaryaṁ*  
*nāśuddhaṁ tac chuddhayati tu mlecchān*

**“The** ***Caitanya-caritāmṛta,*** **the nectarean pastimes of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and the** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **also were translated by you into the language of the** ***mlecchas.*** **What can be more amazing than this? That translation is not impure, but rather is purifying the** ***mlecchas.*****”**

*6. ācaṇḍalebhyo ‘pi tvaṁ kṛṣṇa-prītiṁ*  
*deśe deśe nirvicāro dadāsi*  
*uddhāra-hetus tvad anyo hi nāsti*  
*tasmāt prabhupāda-padaṁ prapadye*

**“Even up the to the dog-eaters \[such as this lowly subhuman\], you are distributing love for Kṛṣṇa. From country to country, without discrimination, you give. Other than you there is no opportunity for deliverance from this material world. Therefore I surrender myself unto Śrīla Prabhupāda’s lotus feet.”**

*7. yukta-vairāgyeṇa nyayojagad yo*  
*yantrāṇy atyādhunikāni hi yāni*  
*brahmārpaṇaṁ sarvatra śikṣā-dātā*  
*taṁ prabhupādaṁ mahantaṁ prapadye*

**“He engaged everything in Kṛṣṇa’s service by the principle of** ***yukta-vairāgya*****, even the most modern of machines. He taught everywhere to offer everything to the Supreme. Therefore I offer myself to him, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the great personality.”**

*8. bhavantam adyāpi na jñātuṁ śakyo*  
*durāvasthānād ahaṁ bhakty-abhāvaḥ*  
*tvaṁ dehi sevāṁ tava pāda-padme*  
*kṛtārtho ‘haṁ syām ataḥ kim iccheyam*

**“O regulative principles, I have not yet been able to understand your glories fully. I am situated so far from you due to having no devotional qualifications. Please give me service to your transcendental lotus feet. Then my life will be successful. Other than this, I desire nothing.”**

*Śrī Māyāpur-candrodaya Mandira –Devotee Roster*

## Melbourne

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Although you have now gone so far away,  
You have not really gone so far away at all.

Just like Kṛṣṇa says He’s far and near,  
But near to those who want to hear.

So you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have come to this cold and dim dark place,  
Where we’re all struggling to exist, this fallen human race.

You have come with a message so pure and sublime,  
And it’s up to us to pick up that line.

In the beginning for some, there may be apprehension,  
But I’m sure in due course, they’ll be full of comprehension.

This is the plan, and you know it quite well,  
The Lord’s desire is to save them all from gliding to hell.

He came Himself to take this work up,  
And you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have never passed the buck.

In India, Caitanya inundated every village and ghat,  
But by His grace you have done better than that.

In all the Western countries the holy name has been spread,  
Now those places will never be the same again.

“Hare Kṛṣṇa” and “Hare Rāma” have become a household phrase,  
Now by your mercy they can get out of the maze.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, what you have done has never been done before!

*Your aspiring servants at Melbourne Mandira*

## Mexico

*Most dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the sanctified dust of your lotus feet. We pray for your infinite mercy because you are everything to us. You are the only solution to the miserable cycle of repeated birth and death.

What can I say to glorify you? What can I do to serve you, to please you? I feel myself to be so small, of so little importance. I hold the pen, wanting to write beautiful words to praise you, to describe your unlimited qualities, and instead hours pass as I remain seated, thinking, and what I am finally able to write seems so insignificant. I want to know how to write the most beautiful poetry to glorify you appropriately, but I find that I am so uselessly dull. Although the Vedic scriptures sing of your character, and your glories are known all over the three worlds, I am so unqualified that I don’t have enough intelligence to properly praise you.

You have everything. What can a conditioned soul like myself offer to you, who one should worship as equal to Bhagavān, Kṛṣṇa? You are His dearest servant and therefore the very manifestation of His mercy, adored by so many disciples in all parts of the world, loved by so many friends, and appreciated and admired by so many scientific and intellectual men. In this way you have constantly shown us how to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. If I could attract your attention if only for a second, I would feel completely satisfied. Therefore, I pray to Lord Kṛṣṇa that he give me the intelligence to be able to satisfy you. I do not ask this to be able to gain riches, beautiful women, or a great number of followers, but only to have the capacity to serve you. If I am unable to satisfy your desire, then I will have the opportunity to return back home, back to Godhead. Y*asya prasādad bhagavat-prasādo yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi.* Therefore, I pray today, and always, in order to have the necessary intelligence to be able to serve you day after day, year after year, birth after birth.

Your fallen disciples of Mexico Yatra

## Miami

*oṁ ajṅāna-timirāndhasya jṅanaṅjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

O divine spiritual master, to even momentarily reflect upon our lives before your presence is a most torturous and frightening experience. The age of Kali is taking its course with meat-eating, illicit sex life, gambling, and intoxication reaching ever-new heights. Being so much in progress, it was obvious that all religious principles had been lost, leaving only darkness in their path. Taking advantage of this situation, so many bogus spiritual leaders were coming forward like glowworms, but because of the dense darkness they appeared to be somewhat luminous and were able to cheat many innocent people. Then by your causeless mercy, you rose like the shining full moon from the east and exposed all those rascals. Not only did you expose the bogus *gurus* but you also exposed the scientists, teachers, doctors, politicians, philosophers and even our own insincere rascal selves. But unlike someone who simply comes forward to point out fault and criticize, you came to administer your shining mercy to everyone and make arrangements for all of them to understand the Absolute Truth and go back home, back to Godhead.

Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you have externally left us, we have actually witnessed your unlimited mercy become even more unlimited. Not only have you remained with us in all of your teachings and your deity form, but now you have further expanded yourself through your many disciples to continue your inconceivable plan of delivering every living entity back to Kṛṣṇa in the spiritual world. Now we are truly able to understand the purport of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura’s poem:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
when thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
to spread the holy name around.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep us in the corner of your lotus feet.

## Mombasa

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

*oṁ ajṅāna-timirāndhasya jṅanaṅjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“Much mercy was thus bestowed upon Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī, and I have briefly described all those topics. Whoever hears this narration with faith and love certainly develops love of God at the lotus feet of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda is my only dream  
In separation it’s hard to have, it seems.  
Jaya Gurudeva is my only desire  
To be delivered from this material fire.

“If one hears the activities of the Lord at the house of Advaita ācārya, he will certainly very soon attain the richest love of Kṛṣṇa.”

All glories to Śrī Guru  
Because of him my life is now true.

“In this way, Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu personally tasted with His own mouth the transcendental qualities of Gopīnātha and Śrī Mādhavendra Purī. One who hears this narration with faith and devotion attains the treasure of love of Godhead at the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.”

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda  
All glories to His Divine Grace  
For introducing us to Lord Viṣṇu  
Who bears in hand lotus, wheel, conch and mace.

“One who hears this narration of Lord Gopāla with faith and love very soon attains the lotus feet of Lord Gopāla.”

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda  
Your glories are not unknown  
Pure love and devotion  
You have shown to Kṛṣṇa

*ISKCON Mombasa at your service*

## Montreal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajṅāna-timirāndhasya jṅanaṅjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Dear and most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura, the great *ācārya* and poet of our Gauḍīya-Vaiṣṇava-sampradāya, has perfectly described the position of the disciple in his complete dependence on the mercy of the Gurudeva:

*yogyatā-vicāre, kichu nāhi pāi,*  
*tomāra karuṇā sāra*  
*karuṇā na hoile, kāṅdiyā kāṅdiyā,*  
*prāṇa nā rākbhio āra*

**“If you examine me, you will find no qualities. Your mercy is all that I am made of. If you are not merciful unto me, I can only weep, and I will not be able to maintain my life.”**

Dear beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is only by your causeless mercy that we are still living, trying to serve your magnificent lotus feet, so dear to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Manohara. We were in complete darkness, lost in a cave without any way out, tied to the walls by the heavy chains of sense gratification and sinful activities. But Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself told you via the transparent medium of your spiritual master to come and deliver us.

It is also said in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.1.14):

*āpannaḥ saṁsṛtiṁ ghorāṁ yan-nāma vivaśo gṛṇan*  
*tataḥ sadyo vimucyeta yad bibheti svayaṁ bhayam*

**“Living beings who are entangled in the complicated meshes of birth and death can be freed immediately by even unconsciously chanting the holy name of Kṛṣṇa, which is feared by fear personified.”**

You opened the locked doors of our dried hearts with the sunshine of your pure words to make us realize that Govinda, your eternal friend, is the only worshipable object of our lives, being the supreme friend, the supreme enjoyer, the supreme proprietor. And then again, by your divine mercy, you also bestow higher kindness to new devotees by becoming their grand-spiritual master. As everybody knows, the grandfather loves his grandchildren more than his own sons. Your love is ever growing in pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness just to shower us with a drop from the ever-expanding ocean of *kṛṣṇa-premā.*

As the dearmost servant of Śrī Puruṣottama, the perfect person, you also reflect the unlimited opulences of beauty and knowledge of the Supreme through all your spotless deeds and words. Your entire life has been a song of glorification, an epic of unparalleled dedication and selfless service to the Supreme and to all mankind for all times. You embody every quality of the pure Vaiṣṇava, the unalloyed servant and spokesman of Vāsudeva. Your divine pastimes have left us awed and eternally indebted to your legacy of perfect knowledge preserved in all your books. Thus, by winding up you manifested pastimes, you have given all our children an impetus to take to heart your potent instructions, the sure road to the transcendental realm, where you are eternally and blissfully serving the supreme friend.

We pray that you bestow your boundless mercy on us, the most wretched and unintelligent, so that we may obtain a glimpse of your unlimited glories. Thus inspired, may we take to heart your perfect instructions, our only shelter and the source of all strength to lead us back home, back to Godhead. All glories to your divine instructions.

*Your unworthy children,*

*the devotees of ISKCON Montreal*

## Murwillumbah

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto his most glorious Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who appeared in this world amidst the influence of the Kali-yuga to deliver the message of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You are the most magnanimous personality. Having appeared in such a hellish time, you came to deliver the whole world from being eternally conditioned. By the mercy of the Supreme Lord you have come and forced, forced, forced open our blinded eyes with the blazing torchlight of transcendental knowledge. O greatest of *mahātmās*, your total surrender to the Supreme Lord paved the path for millions of souls to return home, back to Godhead. You alone have ignited a spiritual revolution destined to spread all over the universe. How could we even begin to glorify your wonderful activities? I must simply begin by begging for your grace so that somehow or other we can push on and spread your mission. In this way your glorious divine personality will be revealed to the world at large.

For millions of births we have been *mlecchas*. In this lifetime we were the most abominable *mlecchas*, committing all kinds of sinful activities. O divine father, our only glitter of hope is to take shelter at your lotus feet, to hear the words emanating from your lotuslike mouth, and to take your instructions as our life and soul.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your smile, your words, your books, these are the only things that matter in our lives. You alone have rekindled our desire to live – not to live in this material world, but live in the eternal kingdom of God. To you, lord and master, we offer our prostrated obeisances crores of millions of times.

Your servants at New Govardhana Farm, Murwillumbah, Australia

**T.S.K.P.**

## Nairobi

*ei-bāre koruṇā koro vaiṣṇava gosāi*  
*patita-pāvana tomā vine keho nāi*  
*yāhāra nikaṭe gele pāpo dūre yāy*  
*emona doyāla prabhu kebā kothā pāy*  
*gaṇgāra paraśa hoile paścāte pāvan*  
*darśane pavitra koro-ei tomāra guṇ*  
*hari-sthāne aparādhe tāre harinām*  
*tomā-sthāne aparādhe nāhi paritrāṇ*  
*tomāra hṛdoye sadā govinda-viśrām*  
*govinda kohena – ‘mora vaiṣṇava parāṇ’*  
*prati janme kori āśā caraṇera dhuli*  
*narottame koro doyā āpnāra boli’*

**“O Vaiṣṇava Gosvāmī, please be merciful to me this one time. You are the savior of the savior of the fallen; without you there is no one. Just by being in your presence sins go far away. Where can anyone find such kindness? Merely by the touch of the waters of the sacred Ganges one becomes liberated, even if he is the lowest of mankind; but just by seeing you, the same effect is achieved. Such is your quality. If one commits an offense at the feet of Lord Hari, he can be forgiven if he cants the holy name. But if one offends you, there is no salvation for him. In your heart Lord Govinda is always resting. Śrī Govinda Himself says, ‘I am the living force of my devotees.’ I hope that in every birth I will obtain the dust of your lotus feet. Śrī Narottama prays, ‘Please, O Vaiṣṇava Gosvāmī, be kind unto me.”**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You have come and appeared before us from the transcendental abode of the Lord. By your holy life, you have exhibited the perfection of pure love for Kṛṣṇa. By your mercy you came to us, who were imbedded in the dark alleyways and cavelike rooms of *māyā’s* illusory prison of material enjoyment, and with the shining transcendental brilliance of your beautiful countenance you blessed us, the lowest among men, with your divine personal association – you, who are a direct associate of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Exhibiting your unlimited compassion, you came to us despite all difficulties and trying circumstances. Across thousands of miles of ocean, for months of discomfort, in pains of old age and disease, yet surpassing all obstacles by your love for Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī and all suffering *jīvas,* you journeyed to us, stooping down to reach us who were helplessly struggling against the material energy as a rat fearfully squirms in the mouth of the powerful cat. Depending fully upon Kṛṣṇa, you came alone, with no assistance from others. But you are the Lord’s pure devotee, therefore you brought Vṛndāvana-dhāma wherever your lotus feet traversed. To fulfill the prediction of Lord Caitanya, you personally took the task and save the most fallen before their sinful reactions took their toll by casting them into a fate of suffering and punishment inconceivable to the human mind. But it will be eternally declared that you saved the world in the time when the grips of impersonalism and voidism crushed the minds and hearts of all human society – even the holy land of Bhārata-bhūmi.

In great meekness and humility, with voice ringing deep into our hearts with the sound of love and compassion, you came into our midst and allowed us to be with you – whose association is sought after even by the great demigods and *ṛṣis.* Out of you mercy only, you walked with us, sat and talked to us, laughed with us and sang with us, ate *kṛṣṇa-prasāda* with us and danced and played musical instruments with us, rode in planes, buses, subways, automobiles, and trains with us. Always with you untinged motive and uninterrupted endeavors you purified us by you association, which was unceasingly and increasingly resplendent with unalloyed *kṛṣṇa-prema*. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, on your every breath there floated the crystal-clear descriptions of the spiritual world, enticing us to set our life’s goal to attain that eternal realm, to which, once going, we would never have to return. By your transcendental voice you simply directly manifested Goloka Vṛndāvana filled with pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the mood of the *vraja-vāsīs*, who eternally serve Kṛṣṇa and know nothing but Kṛṣṇa and the ever-increasing ecstasy of love for Kṛṣṇa. By the unlimited river of nectar coming from your lotus lips, we could understand that there was no other thought than Kṛṣṇa in your white moonlike mind, the abode of pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By your unmotivated devotional activities, which were without cessation observed by all for years on end, twenty-four hours a day, we knew in great pride, ecstasy, and astonishment that in the heart of you, our divine Guru Mahārāja, there was not other desire save and except the pure devotional service of your beloved Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. In the ocean of knowledge that emanated from you, there were perfect and unlimited instructions, descriptions, and examples of nothing other than pure devotional service to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Nanda-nandana. You did not waste time or energy in describing anything but this pure essence of all Vedic knowledge.

There was no situation which you could not perfectly see as being controlled by Kṛṣṇa and to be utilized as an opportunity to serve Kṛṣṇa. You proved to all without a doubt that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Therefore, by all authentic and apparent symptoms, it can be understood by anyone of truthful and honest mind that you are indeed a pure devotee of the Supreme Lord. To find such a personality as Your Divine Grace out of millions and millions of countless living entities within these manifested universes is indeed the most rare chance of incalculable good fortune. Therefore, we can only wonder how it has happened that somehow, by the unlimited causeless mercy of Kṛṣṇa, obtained after wandering throughout practically numberless species of life, we have become so fortunate to come to Śrīla Prabhupāda, to the termination of our journey of suffering in the river of this material world, by coming to the shores of your lotus feet. Let us place our tired souls in surrendered condition at your two beautiful lotus feet and bathe eternally in the cooling shade found there.

There is no other purpose for our existence remaining besides engaging wholeheartedly in the service of the Lord as endowed by you and guided by your all-encompassing instructions.

Your mission has been completed. You have undoubtedly fulfilled the prediction of Lord Caitanya which Your Divine Grace came simply to accomplish. Now we will witness the new era of Bhāgavata-dharma ushered in by your divine appearance. The whole world has already been changed by you. The demoniac forces and atheistic *asuras* have already been defeated by you. Now a world of happiness and prosperity where there is one God, one book, and one religion is shortly to predominate. This is your gift. None of us could have ever imagine your and Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s inconceivable plan to inundate this morbid world with divine love for God. But all along you were perfectly aware of the planned course of events conducted by Kṛṣṇa’s will, as you watched with unflinching faith the development of the branch of Lord Caitanya’s tree, your International Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness. You were always conscious of the course which this *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana* movement would take in its inundation of the whole world. By the desire of the Lord, His pure devotee knows everything and anything necessary to execute His loving service. Therefore, always astounded by your perfect knowledge of what to do in every situation, we simply watched you and followed your instructions blindly as you arranged everything in this International Society for Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the most perfect manner, guiding us through all hardships and threats to our tender devotional creepers, reassuring us through all threats of *māyā* and her agents, setting up the ideal society where all men from all conditions of life can achieve perfection, and thus guiding this transcendental safari on its sure course back to Godhead, despite the barking of the dogs.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, O perfect master, O perfect *ācārya*, please give us the strength to carry on this *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana* movement. Let us be as you are, eternally without any other aspirations or motives. Now you have placed your whole mission – the mission of the *acaryas* in our hands. But we cannot do anything as independent actors. Actually your mercy has made us everything we are, and by your mercy alone we will continue on our sojourn to perfection. We are simply your instruments. Let us dance in perfect step to the unlimited spiritual music broadcast from beyond time and space in your transcendental books.

Now, by your command, each and every one of your disciples must take up the same mood of compassion, sacrificing everything for you, our divine Gurudeva, as you did for your beloved Gurudeva, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, and we must become *gurus*. It was quite inconceivable to us – your intention that we all become *gurus* – but you perfectly understood the desire of the six Gosvāmīs headed by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmīs, and the instructions of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. But now we can understand how the world will be inundated by Lord Caitanya’s movement – as you have quoted to us, reiterating the instruction again and again:

*yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa*  
*āmāra ājñāya guru haṇā tāra’ ei deśa*

**“By my order you become a** ***guru*****, and save this land by telling whoever you see and whoever you meet the instructions of Kṛṣṇa. (Cc.** ***Madhya*** **7.128)**

Yes, you wanted no one *guru*, or two or ten or twenty *gurus,* but thousands and millions of *gurus*, and by your efforts alone (and nobody else will ever be able to claim the credit entitled to Your Divine Grace) you have begun singlehandedly an enormous branch of *śikṣā-gurus* and *dīkṣā-gurus*; and now it will only increase more and more until there are millions of Vaiṣṇavas in every part of the globe. This you said at the Gaura-pūrṇimā festival in 1975 in Śrīdhāma Māyāpur: “Now we have one thousand – so increase this number to ten thousand, then to one hundred thousand, then to one million, then to ten million.” To this transcendental statement of yours there rose a tumultuous roar of cheering, “Jaya Prabhupāda! Jaya Prabhupāda! *Hari bol*!!” filling the entire universe and being witnessed by all of the demigods. Therefore, from the Bhaktivedanta branch of Lord Caitanya’s tree there will be millions of Vaiṣṇava.

Let us not forget for a moment the gravity with which you guided this movement, the serious and always ecstatically joyful mood in which you traveled to all parts of the globe preaching Lord Caitanya’s message. Let us also travel everywhere as you did to preach this message and deliver all of the suffering living entities, forgetting our own comfort. Let us take strength from the words of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura in our effort to take up this mood of preaching exhibited by Your Divine Grace.

*kabe jīve doya, hoibe udoya,*  
*nija-sukha bhuli' sudīna-hṛdoya*  
*bhakativinoda, koriyā vinoya,*  
*śrī-ājñā-ṭahala koribe pracār*

**“When, O when will that day be mine when compassion for all** ***jīvas*** **will manifest in my heart, and with a free heart, forgetting my own comfort, this Bhaktivinoda will set out to preach the divine command?”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have sold ourselves to you as the animal is sold out to its master. Now we have no other duty than to follow your instructions. Our duty is an eternal endeavor to fulfill and unrepayable debt to you. Therefore we should do as you have told us may times, to work cooperatively, cultivating love and trust between all Godbrothers, and thus spread the mission of *śrī-hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana* to destroy all of the demoniac forces of the Kali-yuga. You are eternally watching over us as our loving spiritual father. Therefore, we have nothing to fear. Under the watchful protection of all the Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas*  and the Lord, in the footsteps of your divine lotus feet, we will follow your infallible pathway back to Goloka Vrindavan and there live eternally with you and your beloved Lord Kṛṣṇa.

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam rḍdha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ‘ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caittya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features – externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul – to deliver the embodies living entities by directing them how to come to You.” (*****Bhāg*****. 11.29.6)**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our millions upon millions of *daṇḍavats* at your lotus feet.

## New Orleans

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our fallen obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*  
*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
You’ve shown us how to go to God  
We once were blind and didn’t know  
But now you’ve shown us how to go.

In twelve years you have shown us more  
Than anyone, any time before  
Around the world Kṛṣṇa was unknown  
Now everywhere the seeds are sown.

You’ve shown us how to chant and dance  
Instead of drugs and false romance  
You’ve given us a higher taste  
Instead of making life a waste.

At one time we were cats and dogs  
Pleasures enjoyed even by hogs  
Now all the world they think like this  
Never desiring transcendental bliss.

But we have had a change of heart  
And now with you we’ll never part  
Our love for you is so intense  
We know of nothing so immense.

So now we’ve made the right decision  
To help you fill Caitanya’s mission  
We must make sure your books they get  
At least one book if not a set.

In downtowns, airports or in a lot  
We make sure that a book they’ve got  
And now with sets it’s door to door  
To make sure they’ll have even more.

We sometimes travel town to town  
To save them from their journey down  
To sell your books we go so many places  
Realizing the need (from the looks on their faces).

In every village and in every town  
Kali is there proudly wearing his crown  
Sex! Gambling! Intoxicants! and Meat!  
He rules the whole town on a golden seat.

He can’t believe what he sees when he looks  
For he’s scared to death at the sight of your books  
O Prabhupāda! Your books are so great!  
That Kali is crushed by their virtuous weight.

Quote: “Book distribution marathon is really intoxication”  
But this is going beyond imagination  
As the mode of goodness goes from bitter to sweet  
Now we enjoy going out in the street.

And as we go on pumping books every day  
We find with each book comes a pleasure to stay  
And now we can see that from this book program  
That jīvas are empowered to become superman (see any SKP newsletter).

Because of our book distribution careers  
We know soon devotees won’t be pioneers  
We are confident that this will all come about  
Because of your preaching. Without a doubt!

We know that someday if we just go on  
Kṛṣṇa will come with us on *saṅkīrtana*  
As for the future if we have a choice  
We’ll be your eternal paperboys.

N.B.

Prabhupāda, we are so sorry you have left, but we promise to always follow your instructions, and in that way we know that you’ll always stay with us. Thank you for saving us.

*Your Saṅkīrtana-pura (New Orleans) devotees,*

## New Vrindavan

Śrīla Prabhupāda, feeling your presence  
while we chant, while we work and serve  
understanding that you are not limited  
you are with all of us maintaining our hearts  
with your heaviness against the weight of māyā.

For aeons the soul has been summoned to God’s kingdom  
the refusal has been marked; yet you are  
more kind than Kṛṣṇa Himself; by one moment  
of your association the shackles have been broken  
and now calling us to chant the holy names  
you stand lionlike, spiritual quality  
chasing the jackal of material energy  
away from the pure soul.

Glorious Gurudeva,  
even the words and thoughts are your gift  
we eternally fall short in your company  
you have brought forth the jeweldrop  
of ultimate service from the ocean of immortality  
and having tasted such one’s desire is  
trampling the dry waste of material love  
and now soaring with the intensity of freedom  
we choose the way towards  
the all-attractive Lord.

Through this shelter hold the voices of all  
the great ācāryas and the original  
spiritual master, Kṛṣṇa, is speaking to the  
heart of Brahmā  
Nārada to Vyāsadeva  
and on the banks of the holy rivers  
the thousands of sages assembled to inquire  
from these words  
and now  
as Kṛṣṇa spoke, you are calmly speaking.

O great and powerful spiritual master  
descended from Lord Caitanya’s desire  
A fraction of minute time from the real home  
you stayed  
yet you haven’t left  
for we can see our lives  
entering into the purity of surrender.

Simple is the treasure  
to the submissive yet foolish  
for him the material ocean dries up immediately.

Sleep deeper the fool nonbeliever  
the ocean expands and gains a blacker hue  
by the poisonous rivers emanating from  
his speculative mouth of blasphemy.

While the puppets of sin die in the ages  
strung by the cruel master of time  
their dancing creating deathwebs  
one after another.

We pray to be your dancing puppets of the internal potency  
Singing upon the cooling waves of liberation  
the pastimes of the worshipable Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

May we maintain an attitude of preservation  
taking special care of this presentation of  
the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Help us to keep your instructions as our life  
and to extend them through our tongues and hearts  
to become perfect media of the message  
for it is the Lord directly.

And as you have placed Kṛṣṇa again in our lives  
may we also help give this secret  
to all the conditioned souls and thus they  
can receive the supreme benediction causelessly  
given through the grace of Gurudeva.

*The Inmates of New Vrindaban.*

## New York

*yasya prasādād bhāgavat-prasādaḥ*

In February 1975, Śrīla Prabhupāda entered the temple room of ISKCON Atlanta. He sat on the *vyāsṣana* and started to speak as he looked at the Gaura-Nitāi Deities. All he could say was, “Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda are extremely merciful.” He repeated it and started to cry and left us there in amazement. He couldn’t say anything more. He went back to his quarters and we were stupified. A few days later I asked Śrīla Prabhupāda, “What pleases you the most?” Very dramatically with a voice filled with humility and love he replied, “If you love Kṛṣṇa.” Till this day I am trying to understand the significance of that profound statement. In the *ādi Purāṇa* we find this verse: “Lord Kṛṣṇa told Arjuna, ‘Those who are My direct devotees are actually not my devotees, but those who are the devotees of My servant are factually My devotees.’”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, how do we become your devotee? Śrīla Bhaktivinoda gives the hint. We should try to become like a dog in strictly following the instructions of the spiritual master. When one becomes serious to follow the mission of the spiritual master, his resolution is tantamount to seeing the Supreme Personality of Godhead. A devoted disciple of the spiritual master would rather die with the spiritual master than fail to execute the spiritual master’s mission. Just as Kṛṣṇa comes down to earth to reestablish the principles of religion, so His representative, the spiritual master, also comes to reestablish religious principles. It is the duty of the disciples to take charge of the mission of the spiritual master and execute it properly. In other words, to execute the will of the spiritual master, the disciple should be prepared to lay down his life and abandon all personal considerations.

The mission of the spiritual master is clearly defined. The words *Paramātmā–upakāra* mean “to execute missionary activities for the ultimate benefit of all people.” Lord Caitanya declares,

*ataeva āmī ājñā diluṇ sabākāre*  
*yāhāṇ tāhāṇ prema-phala deha’ yāre tāre*

**“Therefore I order every man within this universe to accept this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and distribute it everywhere.” (*****Cc. ādi*****. 9.36)**

*yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa*  
*āmāra ājñāya guru haṇā tāra’ ei deśa*

**“Instruct everyone to follow the orders of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as they are given in** ***Bhagavad-gītā*** **and** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*****. In this way, become a spiritual master and try to liberate everyone in this land.” (*****Cc*****.** ***Madhya*** **7.128)**

The word *kriyāsu,* meaning “by manual labor” or “by work” can help us to understand the mission of the spiritual master very practically. One should engage in practical service to the Lord. In ISKCON all our activities are concentrated upon distributing *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. This is very important. One may approach any person and induce him to read Kṛṣṇa literature so that in the future he may also become a devotee. Such activities will always remind us of the Lord’s lotus feet. By fully concentrating on distributing books for Kṛṣṇa one is fully absorbed in Kṛṣṇa. This is *samādhi.*

Therefore, the devotees of New York Mandira desire to be most faithful servants of their servants of their spiritual master by being obedient and hard working in executing the mission of the spiritual master, always extolling his qualities and instructions and remembering his glorious pastimes constantly. *Paraṁ vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam.*

## Paris

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Since the day you introduced us to Kṛṣṇa, you have always been very attentive to guide and encourage us to attain His lotus feet. How could we have understood that even your first instructions would continue through the ages, that what you told us then would become our shelter now, and what seemed relative to a certain moment we’d find absolute in time? Even then you spoke of separation and tested our love from far away. In your absence each letter became your arrival, each instructions a divine command. Receiving you in all these ways, your presence came in separation; each new book an eternal reference to your divine self; each lecture to others, our own. Following your words, we lived with you, understanding that though far away you were also near as well. Gradually, as the years went by, we saw you less but knew you more, arriving only at the point that when you left we felt we could go on. Writing us in 1971, you said, “Yes, actually I am present wherever my devotees are engaged in service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Also I am never separated from my Guru Mahārāja. I feel that he is always watching over me and guiding me.”

What was then a momentary reflection now becomes our constant meditation.

Your eternal servants at Paris Temple.

## Paris Farm

On this most auspicious day of the eighty-third year of the appearance of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, it is our great privilege to place a small token of our appreciation and devotion at the lotus feet of him who is none other than the personification of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Your Divine Grace has entered into the affairs of the age of Kali in order to destroy the prevailing ignorance and set the course of humanity for the next ten thousand years. No one can yet appreciate how brightly you literature the lamp of knowledge. You appeared like a full moon through the dense clouds of this age of quarrel and dazed us by your presence. We came to you exhausted, full of distress on the inside and full of false pride on the outside. We could not have gone to anyone else; we had already been through the hands of so many so-called teachers, leaders and philosophers. We were tasting the bitter fruits of frustration. We came to you, attracted by your purity, your utmost simplicity, your honesty and your intimate understanding of the Absolute Truth – qualities which were quickly reflected all over the world in your writings and in your disciples.

In a mere twelve years the face of the world has been transformed. Everywhere signs of the new age of spiritual enlightenment are dawning. The seeds have been planted, the chanting of the holy names is watering everywhere, and you, as a powerful sun, are making everything grow. Faith is reborn, materialistic options are rejected, knowledge is spreading. Peace is entering the hearts of all honest men; humanity like a slumbering giant is slowly coming to its senses again. Signs of hope and joy are everywhere to be seen. How can humanity ever fathom the extent of its debt to you? How can we ever begin to repay you, now that you have gone from our immediate vision as the moon sometimes becomes covered by clouds in the winter season? We lack the vision of faith! We feel like children that have been abandoned by their father. In our helpless condition we prostrate ourselves, and offering our most humble obeisances we pray that you might grant us one last boon: that you, who are always shining brightly within the spiritual world, where you are eternally engaged in unalloyed service to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, might grant us the sincere desire to fulfill your mission. Let this be our heritage, let it be the purpose of our lives: to carry your words to the ends of this world, to be your servants in preparing for the advent of the holy name – Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. Thus we might be allowed to play an insignificant part in fulfilling Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s prediction that the holy names shall resound in every town and village.

## Philadelphia

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. We pray by your grace that we may never deviate from your order and in this way obtain your association forevermore.

We have a copy of a letter you wrote in 1968 to a devotee who had written you that he missed your association. You said:

To feel separation from the spiritual master or Kṛṣṇa is a very good position. That means one who is in pure love with Kṛṣṇa and His representative, the spiritual master, thinks always of them. And this thinking process is Kṛṣṇa consciousness. if we can think always of Kṛṣṇa in separation, that is Kṛṣṇa consciousness. and on the absolute platform, there is no difference of separation and meeting. The separation is also meeting; rather; in separation one relishes the loving relationship more tastily. So don’t be disappointed that you are separated from me; I am also always thinking of you – how you are making progress there.

We are sure you are still thinking of us, and we are thinking of you. “On the absolute platform, there is no difference of separation and meeting,” so this is a great consolation.

But yet, because we never really appreciated you enough while you were here, we are disconsolate. For this, the time for remedy seems past. For some reason or another we were on the same planet at the same time with you, when this most rare and unique opportunity was available – the association of a such a person as you – and we did not sufficiently appreciate it. We shall never again get such an opportunity. Now that you are gone, only now do we understand what we had. We did not love you enough.

And even now we know that you are thinking of us far more than we are thinking of you. Even now we are such scoundrels that we do not appreciate you, that we sometimes become occupied with things not related to your order.

Still there is hope for us. It’s true you have left the planet, but just as truly you have left behind your order for us to execute. If we have just a little faith in you, then we can accept that you are present to us in that order. You have said so. If we can be faithful to that order, we can have your association. We can appreciate that association even if we did not while you were among us.

Caitanya Mahāprabhu said:

Instruct everyone to follow the orders of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as they are given in *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. In this way, become a spiritual master and try to liberate everyone in this land. If you follow this instructions, your materialistic life at home will not obstruct your spiritual advancement. Indeed, if you follow these regulative principles, we will again meet here, or, rather, you will never lose My company. (Cc. *Madhya* 7.128-129)

“You will never lose My company!” We know, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are not affected by death (which is meant only for materialists); and if we are faithful, neither will we be affected. As this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement goes on, we see the proof of this. The materialists question: “How will they be able to go on without their leader?” They are quite right in thinking that we cannot go on without you. We can’t go on for a minute without you. But we *are* going on. Going on only because you are present.

We simply beg your continued mercy. Please grant us the strength to keep your order beating in our hearts. In this way we have your association. In all the good qualities of our Godbrothers and Godsisters we see you. When we see these manifold saintly qualities in them, we remember you, because it is only because of you that they are there. Please grant us the good sense to keep up the association of such saintly persons in your absence. They are your representatives, and you have delivered us into their association. By associating with them we associate with you; please allow us to take solace in such association.

“Liberate everybody in this land!” This is your order. Your order is God’s order. Let us broadcast to the world your stature by the one fitting memorial worthy of you: our country delivered entirely unto your lotus feet. As we love you, we will do it.

We know this will find you in eternal good health.

Your most fallen servants in Philadelphia

## Pittsburgh

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Throughout millions of years and an equal amount of lives we have been searching for the perfect person who could relieve us from the suffering condition of this material world. So many false prophets, teachers, so-called masters and cheaters took us down blind alleys which led only to despair and disillusion. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have not cheated us like all the others. You have given us the highest gift – love of Kṛṣṇa. Out of your causeless mercy you have come here to deliver us rascals who were so full of voidism and impersonalism. Your mercy is all that we are made of. Without your grace our lives would be meaningless.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your kindness. You have taught us everything your glories are spread all over the three worlds. Our lives are dedicated to you; we beg for your mercy. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

*Your eternal servants at New Mathurā,*
