## Portland

**The Most Loving Tribute**

The Passing of the *ācāryadeva* – Śrīla Prabhupāda

*oṁ ajṅāna-timirāndhasya jṅanaṅjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

That sudden morning in November, the day we denied to come – the transcendental disappearance of *nitya-līlā-praviṣṭa* Oṁ Viṣṇupāda 108 Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda Mahārāja. Our eternal father and preceptorial lord brought our proud selves to so many tears and the threshold of the continuing ISKCON movement. Bur for those who are sincere, those same tears simply wetted our undeterminable faith and conviction to push on Śrī Gurudeva’s mission.

Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been kicked out of the nest, empowered by your divine love for God and His creation. O most merciful *gurudeva,* give us at least one tiny drop of your mercy by which we can ignite the spiritual fires in the woodlike hearts of a misdirected civilization. Give us the privilege of becoming your conquering warriors in the difficult fight against *māyā.* Grant us the broad intelligence to clear the field for the seeds of future Vaiṣṇavas.

We were so unlimitedly fortunate to have both your physical and spiritual presence for so many years. For most of us it began by seeing the street *kīrtana*, reading a book or magazine, or some school engagement. We were quickly attracted by Your Divine Grace’s expertly serving us the real sauce – “You are not this body, wake up the real purpose of life …. Chanting, dancing, feasting, so sublime and simple….” The *guru* remained some mystical *buddha* who always spoke with common sense and purely guided our whimsical selves.

As we learned more of the philosophy, we learned of the symptoms of the genuine *guru - śrotriyaṁ brahma-niṣṭham.* He perfectly follows and speaks in an authorized way, and he is the topmost example of one established in spiritual life. Qualities like humility, cleanliness, firm faith in God, etc., denote the purity of the *ācāryadeva*.

From your divine association and service these pure qualities also soon became manifest in our character, which before was degraded by Kali. Devotees became mature and expert in dealings with the material world. We learned what dedication really meant as we awkwardly undertook the critical responsibility of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Following in your lotus footsteps, so many devotees became great men, and yet you also graced those same great men with the humility needed to achieve Kṛṣṇa’s favor. Even Anantadeva, master of the three worlds, cannot describe the glories of your transcendental fame. So many devotees, so many translations of the books, and your unbounded kindness, encouragement, and intelligence.

So now, our beloved Jagad-guru, keep us engulfed in the antiseptic fire of preaching Lord Caitanya’s cult. Do not allow our passionate minds to wander to concocted ISKCONs, to become complacent and materialistic. Please kill this tendency. We lowly beg with sincere prayers that you will keep us under your protection. Do not allow quarreling and mundane speculation to disturb the rudder of Lord Gaursundara’s salvation ship.

*kabe heno kṛpā, lobhiyā e jana, kṛtārtha hoibe, nātha!*  
*śakti-buddhi-hīn, āmi ati dīn, koro more ātma-sātha*

**“When will such mercy fall to this one who is weak and devoid of intelligence? Allow me to be with you.” (Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda)**

*Your most insignificant servants,*

Kaṁsāri dāsa / Bharata dāsa / Ramyaka dāsa / Anubhāvānanda dāsa / Anantātmā dāsa / Edhanīya-svabhāva dāsa / Bhakta Tim / Bhakta Jim / Sukadeva dāsa – Jagamohinī-devī dāsī, Madhurya-līlā, Līlāmādhava, Abhayacaraṇa / Siṁha dāsa - Sāradā-devī dāsī, Matsya / Mahā-bhārata dāsa - Sikhaṇdī-devī dāsī, Sītā-Rāma / Aja dāsa – Abhayā-devī dāsī, Vṛndāvana / Bādarāyaṇi dāsa – Mālādhārī-devī dāsī / Girijārta dāsa – Śrī –matī-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇapriyā-devī dāsī, Śānti / Antara dāsa – Śrīdā-devī dāsī, Nārada Muni, Sanātana / Parama-karuṇa dāsa – Kiśorī-devī dāsī / Mother Reni, Viṣṇupriyā / Strītamā-devī dāsī / Yuvatī-devī dāsī / Bahubhāvanī-devī dāsī

## Rome

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.”**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñanañjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.”**

*akṣṇoḥ phalām tvādṛśa-darśanaṁ hi*  
*tanoḥ phalām tvādṛśa-gātra-sañgaḥ*  
*jihvā- phalām tvādṛśa-kīrtanaṁ hi*  
*sudurlabhā bhāgavatā hi loke*

**“O devotee, the purpose of the eyes is to see you, the purpose of the touch is to touch you. The purpose of the tongue is to glorify you, because in this world it is very difficult to find a pure devotee like you.” (*****Hari-bhakti-sudhodaya*** **13.2)**

We are most unfortunate that we are now bereft of your physical presence, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and even more unfortunate that we never had the opportunity (for many of us, not at all). But, in another sense we are fortunate, because we have always had to be dependent upon your divine instructions. And certainly now, no adherence to your instructions means no association with you. That is only fair. Your physical presence was a luxurious delicacy which we never deserved anyway. It is an opportunity lost; we can no longer hope. How we would become overwhelmed with your kindness when you would answer our questions. How our hearts broke when you spoke of your departure and we realized that we had not yet done anything substantial for you.

Now we must be serious to attract your divine attention, so that when it comes our turn to leave, we will again touch your lotus feet and see you face to face. That is our hope anew.

*Dedicated to your service eternally,*  
*your devotees in Italy.*

## San Diego

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We beg to offer our prostrated obeisances time and time again at your lotus feet, for you have given us the eyes to see how the witch Māyā reigns supreme over the conditioned souls in this world. How, by her spell, people take great delight inflicting one another with mental pain. How they are destroying their planet with poison and neglect. How they’ve squandered their vitality in sexual pursuits. How they’re driven insane by envy and greed. How they’re killing one another without regret. How they feast shamelessly on their brother’s flesh.

Yes, how great we now see is the spell of this deluding witch, Māyā. But how much greater, you’ve also shown, is the shade of your invincible lotus feet, for that shade has given us full shelter from the totality of all these barrages of illusion.

Yes, complete shelter. Never would any of us thought that possible, for it was misery and confusion that we had all finally established as the ultimate reality of this world. In ignorance, we had all grown up wanting to fulfill, at any cost, all of our ludicrous dreams, thinking that they would bring us unlimited satisfaction and joy. As children we’d daydream of this world being vacant of all its people except for “ME”, with everything in it at “MY” disposal. As we “matured” we’d think of having an ideal relationship with the ideal bride and ideal family. Then, great fame and lordship over all. On and on our longings persisted. But even with some apparent successes, our minds were never pacified and our hearts were always pained.

Then you came, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to force yourself upon us, to show us that it was actually your very self who is the very fulfillment of all those wild dreams and desires, those vast longings and distant hopes. You are the last dream we’d have though we wanted to come true. But you have now come to us and fulfilled all our aspirations for happiness simultaneously. No more do we dream of the future. No longer do we hover on the mental plan of wishful thinking. Now we realize that our fortune cannot be calculated by any material consideration. Now we see that we are more fortunate than untold numbers of living entities, because we’ve gained the association and service of the most confidential servant of the Supreme Lord. Therefore, now we cannot leave your feet, lest we again be trapped by the countless illusory conceptions of ultimate fulfillment.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are yours; we cannot belong to anyone else, for you are our lord and master. All we know is what you’ve taught us. All we speak is what we have heard from you. All we do is what you’ve shown us. Therefore, we are considered alive only because you have made us alive. Without you we are dead men only, bereft of everything. Therefore, you are our life and soul, the life of our lives. Turning away from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is walking directly into the hands of death.

## Santiago

We remember realizing once while looking at your lotuslike form, “This person sometimes appears not to be here and sometimes he appears to be here, as if ascending and descending at his will.” Why are his eyes always wet with tears? What great emotion is he feeling? That is hidden from mundane vision. There must be marvelous things behind his external appearance. He carried Kṛṣṇa wherever he went and made everyone surrender, at least a little, to His supremacy. Sometimes Śrīla Prabhupāda would become bored that there was no philosophical competition, that no one could challenge him. He is the greatest, the king of preachers. Feeling his presence or repeating his words, we live with him through his instructions. This king of preachers is so great that not in thousands of years could anyone do so much. Christianity took so long, but look what he has done in such a short time. *Pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma sarvatra pracāra haibe more nāma.* \[*CB Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126\] You are the embodied preaching potency feature of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are happy to offer our heads at your lotus feet, asking that you allow us to become an instrument of that same spiritual potency, and as you have done, give Kṛṣṇa to everyone.

## Santo Domingo

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

In the *Vāyu Purāṇa* an *ācārya* is defined as one who knows the import of all Vedic literature, explains the purpose of the *Vedas*, abides by their rules and regulations, and teaches his disciples to act in the same way. Only out of his immense mercy does the Personality of Godhead reveal Himself as the spiritual master. And now that transcendental professor of spiritual science has become none other than you. We just cannot understand your exalted position.

“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features – externally as the *ācārya* and internally as the Supersoul – to deliver the embodied living beings by directing them how to come to You.”

Since once cannot visually experience the presence of the Supersoul, He appears before us as a liberated devotee. Such a spiritual master is none other than Kṛṣṇa Himself. It is not possible for a conditioned soul to directly meet Kṛṣṇa, but if one becomes a sincere devotee and seriously engages in worshiping you, that thing becomes possible.

It is the conclusion of all sages and scriptures that you should be considered to be directly the Supreme Lord because you give transcendental knowledge for enlightenment. And it is our great fortune that now we have become your disciples without really knowing your divine position. That is your kindness and mercifulness.

However, unfortunately, without the potency to realize what you have given to us, we, being greedy and driven by senses due to our strong bodily desires, have become hesitant and irreverent to your orders. That is the way of the conditioned souls. Therefore, at this time we are humbly begging you one more time to kindly excuse us for all these offenses, and please keep us always protected from the influence of *māyā* under your divine lotuslike feet.

Your unworthy servants of ISKCON Santo Domingo

## Seattle

**On Serving in Vāṇī**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*On this auspicious day of your appearance in this world, we, your disciples, assemble in hearts and minds to offer what we find in them to you. As men, women and children, we assemble here at your lotus words and endeavor to live.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest father, your books are our shelter, your words are our strength. Our lives are dedicated to you and your teachings. The subtle war of mind and senses is fought daily by each man, woman and child in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your instructions are that we must become Kṛṣṇa conscious and always somehow or other think of Kṛṣṇa. In our contaminated state, we hold tightly to the *Bhagavad-gītā.*

**“The senses are so strong and impetuous, O Arjuna, that they forcibly carry away the mind even of a man of discrimination who is endeavoring to control them.” (Bg. 2.60)**

**“One who restrains his senses and fixed his consciousness upon Me is known as a man of steady intelligence.” (Bg. 2.61)**

**“One who is not in transcendental consciousness can have neither a controlled mind nor steady intelligence, without which there is no possibility of peace. And how can there by any happiness without peace?” (Bg. 2.66)**

**“As a boat on the water is swept away by a strong wind, even one of the sense on which the mind focuses can carry away a man’s intelligence.” (Bg. 2.67)**

We want you to know that the sounds of the *Gītā* are penetrating our consciousness. At first, we thought studying *ślokas* was the purpose of the *Gītā*, then to distribute it to others. We are now realizing, dear father, we must live the *Gītā* at every moment. Serving you in *vāṇī*, we are beginning to see the transcendental war against *māyā* as the only reality for the fighting spirit soul. Your words warn us:

“From wherever the mind wanders, bring it back.”  
“The mind is both friend and enemy, restless and unsteady …”  
“A cause of bondage and liberation.”  
“A constituent of the body.”  
“One of the eight separated energies.”  
“A sitting place for lust.”

We are learning (by living our lives performing devotional service to Kṛṣṇa and serving your lotus words) the meaning of the “human form of life”, the value of this form to the living entity in the heart. We are learning that even if Kṛṣṇa takes away everything, we are still perfectly equipped to attain self-realization, having obtained this human body, after sojourning in 8,400,000 species of life. *Caitanya* means “living force”, and Lord Caitanya’s concern is the living entities’ spiritual advancement. His concern, the concern of the great *ācāryas* and your beloved concern has brought us to the realization of our own concern not just for ourselves but for others who have acquired the human body, the most valuable form of life.

The words on your pages are becoming our understanding of “things as they are.” We are endeavoring now to hear and then study your words, dear father, for they contain the essence, the truth of our existence. We are praying constantly to you for your guidance across this ocean of grief and death. We want you to know our hearts are softening due only to knowledge given by you in words that are written for all mankind. Soul, heart, mind, boy, false ego, intelligence – they used to be only empty sounds that had little or no meaning to us, but you filled each one with meaning and truth and applied them like bandages to our confusion. We are growing to love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you gave us the truth which we experience every moment of this sojourn of our lives. We are finally “living” entities.

We love your words, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because they sort us out, give us direction and solution at every step. They are spiritual words and transcendental, and as we enter into each sound, we find you patiently waiting. Then the words take on new meaning, for they are filled also with your dearness to Kṛṣṇa and the greatness of your own heart. O beloved guide in this dark place of material existence, we find you constantly there in your words recorded on pages, on tapes and held forever in our hearts and minds. By them we are “bound” for Godhead. By them, our hearts and minds are stilled and purified. Your words speak of the beauty of the loving Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa. They say Kṛṣṇa is wonderful, wonderful Kṛṣṇa. He is the Supreme Person. And we are inspired, and our thoughts dwell and rest on each one of the glories of God. Our hearts go soft upon hearing of His gentle loving nature, His strength, His complete goodness and His concern for us. “To those who surrender unto Me and worship Me with love, I give them the understanding by which to come to Me.” We are learning, dearest among living entities, that to fight for love is a war that brings real peace. You gave us His name to call upon, His pastimes to worship with our minds and to drink deeply from daily and fill our hearts. He is Hṛṣīkeśa, and you have explained so dearly to us how and where He exists and how we can attain Him.

O dearest spiritual father, you gave us everything … His name is Kṛṣṇa. We are distributing His name and these words to everyone, out of gratitude to you, O dearest of all, because it is your desire. There are many sounds recorded on many pages that we have not yet associated with and are not yet ready to perceive the full meaning of, but they beckon us onward on this path of love for God. We know in due course of time that we will taste the nectar of all your words, and loving Kṛṣṇa will become our natural state. Your words have melted into our lives like falling rain into a puddle, and we will realize the sacred trust of your books of knowledge and causeless mercy of your love for us, who are so fallen. We are unworthy, dear father, of your kindness, your patience, and your love.

Your sons and daughters in devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, who will continue to strive to make others aware of the “human” mission by learning ourselves.

## South Africa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O sublime Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our most unworthy obeisances. We humbly beg you to keep us forever fixed in constant devotional service at your lotus feet, for, as the Supreme Lord’s pure representative, you are truly our lord, birth after birth. Simply by giving aural reception to the transcendental sound coming from your lips, the dormant *prema-bhakti* is awakened in our hearts, and by your mercy alone the vast ocean of material suffering becomes a puddle of water in the hoofprint of a calf.

To offer such words to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, most swanlike incarnation of compassion, is like offering a glass bead to one who possesses millions of the rarest jewels, but by Lord Caitanya’s mercy, we might be able to convey the feeling of love that we have for you. Before we received your mercy, we were like deserts. We had not a drop of love, neither for ourselves nor for others. Indeed, we were the possessors of all bad qualities and were totally selfish, not even knowing the nature of the true self, yet by you we were drowned and overflooded in an ocean of all-consuming and ever-increasing love. So our love, our dearmost *ācārya*, is now what we are offering you. You have cultivated it in our hearts, and its flowers we are offering to you and humbly petitioning you to accept them. Otherwise our lives become meaningless.

When we read the beautiful prayer written by Your Divine Grace on your arrival in America, what can we do other than love you? For when you perfectly carried out the instruction of your Guru Mahārāja you showed a compassion for us fallen souls, the like of which has never been seen in the West before or since. We remember how you wrote, “Most of the population here is covered by the material modes of passion and ignorance. Absorbed in the material life they think themselves very happy and satisfied, therefore they have no taste for the transcendental message of Vāsudeva. I do not know how they will understand it.” A later verse continues, “I wish that You may deliver them. Therefore, if You so desire their deliverance, then only they will be able to understand Your message.” No greater examples of true compassion exist in Vaiṣṇava literature than these verses. Surely no pure devotee ever worked with more degraded people, coming from a hellish society in which nearly every individual is a Jagāi and Mādhāi, and yet since you are Lord Krṣṇa’s dearmost devotee, thousands of these same fallen souls are now engaged by your mercy in His transcendental loving service.

Lord Caitanya has crowned you His beloved devotee with all success, and He has empowered you to spread the chanting of the holy names over an area even greater than He did during His own divine *līlā,* even though our love is so limited and we are bereft of all intelligence, we can understand that you are always with us to the extent that we follow your loving instructions, and the light of your instruction illumines every footstep of the way on this royal road of *bhakti*. Our striving in our small way to spread Krṣṇa consciousness in South Africa pleased you very much in the past, and we realized, by your special interesting this area, that somehow, if our plans to spread Krṣṇa consciousness in the future bring you pleasure, that this is the fulfillment of our lives.

*Your fallen servants,*

## Sri Lanka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāni-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāśatya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We, who are the lowest of the world, fall down at the dust before your lotus feet on this auspicious day, Śrīla Prabhupāda! We are overcome with mixed joy and remorse on celebrating your appearance day and by remembering your untimely departure. You so kindly descended from within the circle of Krṣṇa’s most confidential associates into our own midst. You showed us the way back to Godhead. Now you have returned to your eternal *līlā* with Krṣṇa. If we want to again enjoy your transcendental association, we have no other alternative than to fully traverse the perfect path of *bhakti* taught by you.

Previously, we were being directly guided by you. This process is technically called *śravaṇa-kīrtana. Śravaṇa* means aural reception from the superior spiritual authority, and *kīrtana* means to act according to what one has heard. When one hears the philosophy of Krṣṇa consciousness and wholeheartedly takes it up, the next stage will automatically follow – that is called *smaraṇa*, or remembering. This *smaraṇa* means first of all to always remember the instructions of the *guru,* then one will naturally remember Krṣṇa. How perfectly we have practiced *śravaṇa-kīrtana* will now be tested by how perfectly we can effectualize *guru-vāṇī-smaraṇa.*

One day in 1969, you were sitting in your room at 26 Second Ave., New York. There were only five devotees present, and you were explaining the significance of the *guru-paramparā.* you said, “The chain of disciplic succession is just like an electric wire. The power is coming down from the powerhouse through the wire right into your room, so that you can plug into it. There may be so many holes in the wall, but if you contact them you will not get any result. If, however, you touch the bona fide outlet, you will immediately feel the power. That is the proof – you will immediately feel that the power is there. I am such a bona fide outlet.”

We are thankful for having heard these words from your lotus lips and we pray that we may yet be able to fully realize them by completely surrendering to you.

*All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!*

Rohiṇī-kumāra dāsa brahmacārī / Mahākartā dāsa brahmacārī / Krṣṇa-Balarāma dāsa brahmacārī / Ūrdhvaga dāsa brahmacārī / Ripughna dāsa brahmacārī / Acyutāgraja dāsa brahmacārī / Kapiladeva dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Rājā / Bhakta Viṣṇudāsa / Nandarāṇī-devī dāsī

## Stockholm

We have no qualifications to write something about such a person as Śrīla Prabhupāda. Whatever we might note down would not come near presenting his true magnificence and personality. For us, Śrīla Prabhupāda is everything. He is our lord and master. He is our father and friend, our sympathizer and rectifier.

The great transcendentalists take birth in the families of devotees. From their very birth the names of Lord Krṣṇa are being sung in their presence. The great Vaiṣṇava grow under the protection of the Supreme Lord, and when they are ready they bless the whole world with their high standard of pure love of Krṣṇa. And then, when Krṣṇa so desires, the devotee returns to Him to engage in His intimate eternal pastimes of pleasure.

We, with our mundane vision, can hardly have a glimpse of the magnitude of character of a pure devotee as our Śrīla Prabhupāda. The name *Prabhupāda* itself signified “that personality who all other great masters surrender to.” Śrīla Prabhupāda’s lotus feet have become the only shelter of the world. If anyone in this world wants to get free from the inferno of material existence, he must approach the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda through the *paramparā.*

There is no difference between *guru* and Krṣṇa in the sense that the *guru* is the pure representative of Lord Śrī Hari. Śrī Kṛṣṇa is coming to us through the medium of the spiritual master. In the beginning, there was only Kṛṣṇa in this universe. Then He created Lord Brahmā and empowered him with transcendental vision to create the living entities and educate them about becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. Kṛṣṇa gave Himself through Lord Brahmā, the spiritual master in disciplic succession is Kṛṣṇa manifesting Himself before us.

Then there was Nārada, Brahmā’s son, who carried the message all over the universe. Śrī Nārada was especially expert at delivering the gross materialists who were attached to fruitive work. He taught them how to work in a devotional spirit and thus attain perfection, love of God.

Then there was Vyāsa, the all-knowing incarnation of the Supreme Lord, who could foresee all the difficulties of the people of Kali-yuga. Out of compassion, he compiled the Vedic teachings into books. Then there was Śrī Madhvācārya, the great learned leader and stalwart preacher then there was Mādhavendra Purī, who introduced *mādhurya-rasa* in the line of devotion. Then there was Lord Caitanya, who is Himself the supreme original personality Govinda. He came to earth to experience the mood of Rādhārānī, and by so doing flooded the whole world with tears of ecstasy in pure love of Krṣṇa.

Then there were the six Gosvāmīs, whose mission it was to perfectly record and present in precious scriptures the intent and feelings of Lord Caitanya. Out of compassion, they made the intimate understanding of Lord Caitanya possible for all. Then there was 108 Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṇsa Parivrājakācārya His Divine Grace Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who took the message of the Gosvāmīs and carried it to the ends of the earth.

The same Krṣṇa that inspired Brahmā, Nārada, and then Byāsadeva and Madhva; the same Lord Gopīnātha who saved sweet milk for Mādhavendra Purī and who caused Īśvara Purī to experience constant ecstasy; the same Gopījanavallabha who came as Lord Caitanya and inspired Rūpa and his followers; that same Krṣṇa fully blessed our Śrīla Prabhupāda to present His very Self to those civilizations which had long fallen from the Vedic life.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day, we bow to him who is the representation of the will and order of Śrī Krṣṇa and who is Śrī Krṣṇa’s most intimate representative: 108 Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṇsa Parivrājakācārya His Divine Grace Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. We prostrate ourselves before our infallible lord with tears of separation wetting our heartfelt vows of eternal servitude.

## Switzerland

*tvam nah sandarsito dhatra dustaram nistitirsatam*  
*kalim sattva-haram pumsam karnadhara ivarnavam*  
*(Bhag. 1.1.22)*

Dear Srila Prabhupada,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your divine feet. I do not know how or where to begin appropriate glorification of your lordship. I am a miserable fool quite unfit to serve your lotus feet or sing your praises, but I must confess that I have no other attraction.

Out of your compassion, you came to this material world where we were wandering aimlessly from one body of misery to another, and you delivered the absolute science: that Lord Sri Krsna, who appeared in Vrajabhumi (Vrndavana), is the Supreme Personality of Godhead and is therefore worshipable by all. Your message has been heard and has penetrated to the core of our heart.

Some, may complain that you were with us for only a few fleeting and insufficient moments. Others may claim that they did not obtain enough of your personal association. Still others may lament that your departure was "untimely," that we were not "ready" for you to leave.

What was our claim to have even met you? To have become your servants? To have received even once—what to speak of many times—your personal instructions? Or to have captured your merciful glance? None. We dare not view your glorious pastimes through the prism of our imperfect senses and mind. Like the Supreme Lord Sri Krsna, whose pure and perfect devotee you are eternally, you are beyond our empiric area or experiencing jurisdiction. "The materialistic demeanor cannot possibly stretch to the Transcendental Autocrat who is ever inviting the fallen conditioned souls to associate with Him through devotion of eternal serving mood," you proclaimed sublimely, as you ever invited *us* to associate with *you* through devotion of eternal serving mood.

The value of a moment's association with such a pure devote as yourself bestows *sarva-siddhi,* or all perfection, upon us. Such a moment, Srila Prabhupada, is better than crores of years on the heavenly planets or eons of so-called liberation in the Brahman, what to speak of the customary pleasures which are available to the dogs and stool-eaters. "Fleeting" means that which comes momentarily and then, like bubbles in the ocean, passes away irretrievably. Rather than being fleeting, our association with Your Lordship must be deemed the only thing which is permanent, as we have unconditionally accepted you as the captain of our ship—life after life. While countless numbers of bygone lives of sense gratification and mental speculation have already been condemned to oblivion (who remembers them?), we shall continue our eternal serving mood at your lotus feet, in separation, during the rest of this life and wholly expect to resume your personal service in the next. *Caksu-dana dila yei, janme janme prabhu sei.*

Eternal father, you need not worry that the reservoir of our attachment to you was nourished only by your physical presence amongst us. Even when you were personally present, the devotional service of most of your disciples was regularly executed in loving separation, as Your Divine Grace journeyed overseas for preaching. Several years ago you confided to Hamsaduta Maharaja that the *acarya* remains forever within the material world and journeys from one planet to another for preaching—and that his intimate disciples accompany him. The *Bhagavatam* describes each planet within the universe as a *dvipa,* or an island surrounded by the "ocean" of space. On behalf of Lord Caitanya's mission, you have simply extended the preaching further overseas than ever before. Somewhere you are lecturing on *the janmady asyayatah* verse of *Srimad-Bhagavatam,* taking your morning walk, and founding another 1SKCON temple. Therefore, you are still with us in the same way as before—and always will be. Actually, as there is no separation between fire and air, so there is no separation possible between your lordship and your servants. "Uddhava continued: These are the words from the Personality of Godhead, 'My dear *gopis.* My dear friends, please know that separation between ourselves is impossible at any time, at any place, or under any circumstances.' "

I offered a*rati* to Your Lordship shortly before your departure from our mundane eyes. I could see that you were tolerating bodily discomforts far greater than any of your disciples could imagine. I could understand that you might leave our midst, but I could not believe it nor could I accept it. I know why. You *cannot* leave us. You *did not* leave us. That is why. *Desa-kalartha-yuktani hrt-tapopasamani ca haranti smaratas cittam govindabhihitani me:* "Now I am attracted to those instructions imparted to me by the Personality of Godhead \[Govinda\] because they are impregnated with instructions for relieving the burning heart in all circumstances of time and space." *(Bhag.* 1.15.27) I only know that somehow or other-through no merit of my own—Your Divine Grace has allowed me to come into the shade of your lotus feet, which you will never withdraw and from which I never wish to leave. You live forever by your divine instructions and your followers live with you.

The *guru* is as much with the disciple as the disciple wants him to be. Thus, Providence may allow one to come close to Your Divine Grace physically, but if one is not your sincere servant, you still may not be with that person. Similarly, for the sincere devotee your constant company is assured, for in the core of his heart that devotee has erected a throne on which you gladly sit. Such a faithful disciple can never neglect you or your instructions, nor can he ever be neglected by you. He remains with you as your eternal associate.

This is my only wish. O son of Maharaja Nanda! No matter how hard I cry, please do not fulfill any of my desires, save one. Allow me to always assist my lord and master, Srila Prabhupada, in any menial way. You may deem appropriate, and thus become his intimate associate. "I am not at all afraid of the thunderbolt of King Indra, nor am I afraid of the serpentine, piercing trident of Lord Siva. I do not care about the punishment of Yamaraja, the superintendent of death, nor am I afraid of fire, scorching sun, moon, wind, nor the weapons of Kuvera." Neither do I fear being denied entrance to Vaikuntha. Yet, I am terrified that I may forsake your sacred devotional service. Catch hold tightly of this puny servitor. He has eternally sold his head at your lotus feet and never wishes to be released by you.

*The devotees of ISKCON Switzerland.*

## Sydney

Dear Srila Prabhupada,

Please accept our most fallen and humble obeisances at your lotus feet. By your grace alone we can cross the ocean of material suffering and obtain the mercy of Krsna. All glories to you, O Gurudeva, whose fame is spread all over the three worlds.

Living in the material world is like being in a hurricane. In a hurricane everything is disturbed. Trees are uprooted—this may be compared to the breaking of temporary ties of friendship and love in human family and social life. Houses are destroyed—even the most sturdy house can be destroyed in a hurricane; similarly, even the sturdiest body is destroyed by the winds of time. Crops are flattened, just as the desires of the living entity for material enjoyment are ultimately flattened by death. Right into the midst of the roaring hurricane you have fearlessly stepped with the shelter of the holy name to offer invincible protection and clear directions for the path back home.

Krsna sent you to us in this wintry age and now, by His infinite mercy, He has taken you back again. The mundaner will say that you have passed into oblivion, but they have yet to taste the nectar of your divine words. Gone from sight you may be, but still in sound you reside with us. Nay, still in form for one who has the eyes to see you in your *murti.*

So, beloved Gurudeva, like an expert physician you have prescribed the remedy for all life's ills. We can never pay the fee and so will always be beggars in your debt. At this time let us simply reassert that we will continue with all our energies to push on this *sankirtana* movement to every town and village. Let that be for your glory.

*We beg to remain your menial servants, eternally in the shelter of your lotus feet.*

## Tehran

Dear Srila Prabhupada,

Please accept our humble obeisances millions of times in the dust of your lotus feet. Our beloved master, father, guide and best friend, you have now completed your perfect pastime of delivering countless souls from the darkness of illusion. You have expanded your service and returned to your eternal *lila,* leaving us saddened by your personal absence. But by your genius you have left us so much to do, so many instructions, books, and great goals to achieve, that we can still feel your presence through our busy endeavors to spread Krsna consciousness. Your personal example, however, has left a permanent impression in our hearts from which to continue to draw our inspiration to serve the Supreme Personality of Godhead. We are only beginning to find out a little bit of what you mean to us. Please bless us forever with your remembrance. We are exasperated by your separation. How we would love to give you a guided tour of our insignificant projects and catch the nectar drops of criticism or kindly appreciation from your lotus lips. We want to wash your lotus feet and to offer you all kinds of *prasada,* especially the local fruits which you always loved. We remember with pleasure the encouragement and example you gave us for our preaching work during your visits to Iran. How patiently you were spending so many hours interviewing our Muslim friends, revealing the art of how to preach according to time and circumstance. Now there are so many more interested people, and we always imagine what it would be like to introduce them to you and to have you charm them into performing devotional service.

Srila Prabhupada, always resonating in our intelligence are the two themes you always stressed for our program here. One of these is the universality of the *bhakti* cult as the culmination of all religious systems. You encouraged and praised the Islamic chanting of Allah on *tasbikh (Japa* beads) and the public chanting of the *azan* from the mosques at sunrise, noon and sunset. On morning walks you extolled the simple piety of the common people, their dress and their courtesy. Never did you write them off as hopeless or as demons. You painted for us a broad and compassionate picture of the people surrounding us, giving us confidence that they could and would understand our message if we were determined and patient. You made us see and appreciate the value of the traditional social and political systems practiced here, rather than rejecting entirely the people, religion, government and culture, as we tend to do. Now we hope to preach in this way, always fixed on our goal of delivering the pure message of Lord Caitanya.

The other theme which you emphasized for us was simple but important for the genuine growth of our movement. It is, as was taught to Arjuna in the *Bhagavad-gita,*

*yad yad aarati sresthas tat tad evetaro janah*  
*sa yat pramanam kurute lokas tad anuvartate*

**"Whatever action is performed by a great man, common men follow in his footsteps. And whatever standards he sets by exemplary acts, all the world pursues."**

To make the great men around us truly great and to set an example for them in Krsna consciousness is our specific desire here in Tehran. In order to accommodate this preaching mode, our devotees, with your approval, hold positions in society or engage in regular work. We beg your special protection because we must forego the protection of Vaisnava dress and continual devotee association. We cannot falter or deviate from the principles of Krsna consciousness, as a small mistake will carry great costs in this preaching field. Our results, though still small, are the respect, appreciation and participation of older, influential and responsible people in society. This is your desire—to present Krsna consciousness to the intelligent class of men. They can put it into effect for everyone's benefit. A man can cause an avalanche of love of God. You have shown us the right stone: the intelligent and the leaders. And by your example you have shown us how to move it.

We hope to take your example and, fulfilling your desire, inject transcendental knowledge deep into the power structure of modern society. Giving personal inspiration in God consciousness to political leaders; professionals and businessmen is one of the most important functions for *brahmanas* in forming a basis for helping all living entities in general. Our Krsna conscious philosophy will someday be the cultural, political and economic basis of mature and civilized communities and nations. We look forward with enthusiasm to the day when we can show this to you, Srila Prabhupada, as a token of our gratitude to you. How compassionate you are. It is beyond our comprehension. You have given us Krsna's service, and we now only want your pleasure. Please bless us with the strength to carry on your work.

Your humble servants,

Parivrajakacarya Svami / Roelof / Ali I / Nilotfar, Atreya Rsi dasa / Claude / Sederat / Azi / Dayanandadasa / Cerjo / Amir Khosrow / Shahrzad, Navayauvanadasa / Noel / Ali II / Fatameh / Bhakta-rupa dasa / Nimai / Vidhibhakti / Patricia / Divyadhama dasa / Ramananda Raya / Manjari, Marie / Lomasa Rsi dasa / Namacarya Haridasa, Vinoda-vani / Lalita / Bhargadasa / Mohana-rupa / Ekanti / Vanessa / Vallabhadasa, Siamak / Ingrid / Dvijatipujaka dasa, Salim / Christina.

## Toronto

*nama om visnu-padaya krsna-presthaya bhutale*  
*srimate-bhaktivedanta-svamin iti namine*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada, who is very dear to Lord Krsna, having taken shelter at His lotus feet."**

*namas te sarasvate deve gaura-vani-pracarine*  
*nirvisesa-sunyavadi-pascatya-desa-tarine*

**"Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are so kindly teaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are so full of impersonalism and voidism."**

*om ajnana-timirandhasya jnananjana-salakaya*  
*caksur unmilitam yena tasmai sri-gurave namah*

**"I was born in the darkness of ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him."**

*na veda kascid bhagavams cikirsitam*  
*tavehamanasya nrnam vidambanam*

**"O Lord, no one can understand Your transcendental pastimes, which appear to be human and are so misleading."** ***(Bhag.*** **1.8.29)**

Lord Krsna-explains in the *Bhagavad-gita, janma karma ca me divyam,* that both His birth and activities are transcendental. But few could appreciate His appearance under the influence of *yogamaya.* Arjuna explains that even he didn't appreciate Krsna's appearance.

*sakheti matva prasabham yad uktam*  
*he krsna he yadava he sakheti*  
*ajanata mahimanam tavedam*  
*maya pramadat pranayena vapi*

**"I have in the past addressed You as 'O Krsna, O Yadava, O my friend,' without knowing Your glories. ... I have dishonored You many times.... Please excuse me for all my offenses."**

Similarly, Srila Prabhupada, your appearance and disappearance are transcendental. How many times did we offend you, covered by *maya's* influence? Please forgive us for all our offenses.

*saksad-dharitvena samasta-sastrair*  
*uktas tatha bhavyata eva sadbhih*  
*kintu prabhor yah priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroh sri-caranaravindam*

**"The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord on account of his being the most confidential servant of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and is followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bonafide representative of Sri Hari, Krsna."**

How many times did we fall asleep at your feet while you spoke *Srimad-Bhagavatam?* Why didn't we take full advantage of your personal association just as if we were with Lord Caitanya or Lord Krsna? We just couldn't understand your greatness. I don't think we will ever know your greatness; not even in millions of years. You are renowned throughout the three worlds as the greatest preacher of Krsna consciousness. *Ebe yasa ghusuk tri-bhuvana:* "Your fame is spread all over the three worlds." If the Christians had the shoes that Christ had walked in, the clothes he wore, the bed on which he slept, tapes of his "Sermon on the Mount," how treasured they would be. How fortunate we are that you have given us all these and more, including *Srimad-Bhagavatam, Caitanya-caritamrta* and *Bhagavad-gita As It Is,* what to speak of your personal association. When you left, we were initially thinking that there would be no hope for us without your personal presence. Now, by your grace, everything you have given us has taken on a new transcendental meaning. You have mentioned so many times that you are nondifferent from your books and nondifferent from your instructions, but we could not understand. Now that you have left, these transcendental items are coming alive with new meaning and enlivening us to push on your mission. By your mercy, this is all happening to make us more serious. Every time we see your picture, look at your shoes, hear your lotus voice, chant your glories, associate with your devotees, worship your Deities, offer our obeisances, and preach your mission, our love for you increases.

The feelings of separation are undoubtedly painful for us, but because these feelings are for you, Srila Prabhupada, there is a specific transcendental effect which pacifies the heart. That is the way this movement will go on. It is a truly transcendental movement, and you are Krsna's bona fide representative. Feelings of separation are also sources of transcendental bliss, and they are never comparable to contaminated material sentiment.

There are so many "new religions" that appeared in the sixties along with ISKCON. Gradually they will all die out because they are simply cheating processes. But this Hare Krsna movement will go on because of you, Prabhupada. You are Krsna's pure devotee and the true savior of the world. You have injected this movement with your transcendental potency in order to take over the whole world for Krsna and smash agnosticism and atheism. Actually, this movement is a manifestation of your pure desire to serve your spiritual master and Lord Caitanya. It is nondifferent from yourself. It will continue to spread its influence all over the universe. If we remain ISKCON men, we will never lose your precious association. If we are actually sincere disciples, we will never give up your service, no matter what happens in the future years of Kali-yuga. That will be the real test of our understanding of the teachings and mercy you have shown us.

What ordinary preacher could have done what you did? You made *Krsna* a household word throughout the world in twelve short years. You spread His glories to every town and village. Prabhupada, we cannot appreciate your inconceivable mercy upon us. You traveled the whole world and made even the most remote areas into places of pilgrimage.

We recently visited the Toronto airport, and while recollecting your arrival in 1976 and seeing the places where your lotus feet had walked, we wanted to offer full *dandavats.* We are so fortunate that out of billions and billions of conditioned souls rotting in this universe, you, who are so exalted, have chosen us, who are so fallen, as your disciples to carry on your mission and spread Krsna consciousness.

You have given us the opportunity to receive the highest mercy, *krsna-prema.* We can never repay you. But simply let us serve you birth after birth in this ISKCON movement. Let us, as your disciples, take your instructions as our life and soul, worship them and carry them out for the rest of our existence.

Let us cooperate to serve your mission. Take us. Make us your humble servants. All glories to your disciples, our Godbrothers and Godsisters. All glories to your movement, ISKCON. All glories to you, Srila Prabhupada. Thank you for never leaving us alone.

*Your eternal servants at New Remuna Dhama.*

## Trinidad

*Om ajnana-timirandhasya jnananjana-salakaya*  
*caksur unmilitam yena tasmai sn-gurave namah*

*sri-caitanya-mano-'bhistam sthapitam yena bhutale*  
*svayam rupah kada mahyam dadati sva-padantikam*

My beloved spiritual master, Your Divine Grace,

Please forgive this inadequate attempt to offer homage on this most auspicious day of your appearance. At present we are feeling the pangs of separation on your disappearance, but for yourself the two are the same. Because of your desireless attitude, we know we are not competent enough to glorify your character, which is unlimited, being the mercy manifestation of the Supreme Absolute Truth, just as we are not competent to glorify the Lord, who is Uttamasloka.

O my beloved lord and master, please assist your selfish children to be selfless, knowing our goal to be your words and your words to be the desire of Lord Caitanya Mahaprabhu, which is the spreading of Krsna consciousness.

Please, my dear lord and master, give us the strength to be free from this stringent material nature, which illusions great demigods, what to speak of ourselves, to think of "I" and "my," which is the basic disease of the material world.

O beloved master, assist us so we may not fall prey to illusory designations and so we remember that we are all your eternal servants in different statuses. Please give us the strength to remember this.

All glories to you, Srila Prabhupada.

*Your eternal servants at ISKCON Trinidad.*

Tarksya dasa / Krsna-sarana dasa brahmacari / Gopati dasa brahmacari / Siddhanjana dasa brahmacari / Tadakaha dasa brahmacari / Virahadeva dasa brahmacari / Devendranuja dasa brahmacari / Harideva dasa adhikari / Kirtaniya dasa / Bhakta Franklyn / Bhakta Junior / Surottama dasa adhikari / Bhakta Haresh / Bhakta Blues / Bhakta Renny / Bhakta Tony / Jamatri-devi dasi / Mahendrarani-devi dasi / Moksada-devi dasi / Bakula-devi dasi / Vidhutama-devi dasi / Lalita / Bhaktin Goldene.

## Valencia

*nama om visnu-padaya krsna-presthaya bhutale*  
*srimate bhaktivedanta-svamin iti namine*

*namas te sarasvate deve gaura-vani-pracarine*  
*nirvisesa-sunyavadi-pascatya-desa-tarine*

All glories to you! You are the emissary of Lord Krsna on this planet. Therefore, we offer our respectful obeisances once and again. You, Srila Prabhupada, are *patita-pavana,* the friend of the most fallen, the manifested representation of Lord Nityananda Prabhu. Lord Nityananda liberated Jagai and Madhai, but you, Srila Prabhupada, are liberating thousands of Jagais and Madhais. Srila Prabhupada, we are made of your mercy, and out of your mercy we will actually be able to serve you. Please help us to fulfill your desires. *Sri-guru-carane rati ei sei uttama gati.* Just your lotus feet are the perfection that fulfill all desires. If we can keep in contact with your lotus feet, this will cure the disease from which we have been suffering since time immemorial.

Srila Prabhupada, you are the true *guru.* Our obeisances are unto you, a servant of Sarasvati Gosvami; you are expanding and preaching the message of Sri Caitanya. O master, from you emanates ecstatic *prema.* You have filled our hearts with bliss—something we had never felt before—and it forces us to serve your lotus feet. Nevertheless we are not able to serve you; therefore we ask for your mercy to engage us in your service. O Gurudeva, we are forgetful souls, and our desires are attached to the mundane objects. You are very expert in transforming the conditioned living entities' desires and turning them towards Krsna’s lotus feet. Please, shower your mercy on us. O spiritual master, you are just like the morning sun that dispels the darkness of the night. Similarly, with the torchlight of knowledge you enlighten the conditioned souls to liberate them from ignorance. All glories unto you, spiritual master.

O my dear Gurudeva, master of masters, eternal and gracious enlightened soul, you have descended into the depths of the material hell to check all of us fallen souls from drying up, pouring on us the unlimited nectar of the Hare Krsna *maha-mantra.*

Dear Srila Prabhupada, our humble prayers are that you forget all our offenses because of our not being serious in trying to please your desires. We offer our fallen *dandavats* at your most holy lotus feet.

*The devotees of ISKCON Valencia.*

## Vancouver

Jaya nitya-lila-pravista Om Visnupada Paramahamsa Parivrajakacarya Astotara-sata Sri Srimad Abhaya Caranaravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Maharaja Prabhupada *ki jaya.*

"All glories to the *acarya,* Om Visnupada 108 Tridandi Gosvami Abhaya Caranaravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada, who travels across the earth preaching the glories of Hari, who is situated on the highest platform of *sannyasa,* and who has entered into the eternal pastimes of the Lord."

*naivopayanty apacitim kavayas tavesa*  
*brahmayusapi krtam radha-mudah smarantah*  
*yo 'ntar-bahis tanu-bhrtam asubham vidhunvann*  
*acarya-caittya-vapusa sva-gatim vyanakti*

**"O my lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahma, for You appear in two features—externally as the** ***acarya*** **and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You." (*****Bhag.*** **11.29.6)**

*yadyapi amara guru—caitanyera ddsa ,*  
*tathapi janiye ami tanhara prakasa*

**"Although I know that my spiritual master is a servitor of Sri Caitanya, I know him also as a plenary manifestation of the Lord."**

*guru krsna-rupa hana sastrera pramane*  
*guru-rupe krsna krpa karena bhakta-gane*

**"According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Krsna. Lord Krsna in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees." (Cc.** ***Adi.*** **1.45, 46)**

*siksa-guruke ta' jani krsnera svarupa*  
*antaryami, bhakta-srestha,—ei dui rupa*

**"One should know the instructing spiritual master to be the Personality of Krsna. Lord Krsna manifests Himself as the Supersoul and as the greatest devotee of the Lord." (Cc.** ***Adi.*** **1.47)**

Srila Prabhupada! When shall we awaken from the darkness of ignorance to realize your eternally effulgent greatness? When shall we begin to fathom your most exalted position? When shall we constantly hold the remembrance of your lotus feet to our hearts? When shall we free ourselves from these abominable material attachments and take full shelter of your instructions? When shall we appreciate your munificence, in delivering us from the clutches of *maya?*

Srila Prabhupada, please forgive us. We are like small children who are inconsiderate and neglectful of their kind loving father. You have saved us from repeated birth and death in material existence and have awarded us with eternal life. O most merciful spiritual master, please allow us to understand your magnificence and graciousness, and allow us somehow to repay this great debt we have to you by engaging us as your menial servants. Please bless us that we may never fall away from your lotus feet as victims of the material energy.

Srila Prabhupada, please, now that you have left us physically, manifest yourself within our hearts so that we may be guided in carrying out your momentous mission. With our minute intelligence it is difficult for us to grasp the reality of your far sighted plans. Yet we know that they will come to be, and we pray that you will allow us to take part.

In 1965, in your first days in America, you sat on a bench in Tompkins Square, pointed your finger out into the park, and remarked to an early admirer, "We have temples and farms... schools out there. They are there; we simply have to have faith in Krsna and move through time." Although you were almost penniless at that time, it has all come to be. We can only watch the future of your mission unfold itself with great expectations. Please bless and empower us so that we may assist you in your mission.

*mukam karoti vacalam pangum langhayate girim*  
*yat-krpa tam aham vande sri-gurum dina-taranam*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains."**

May our life and soul be eternally dedicated to satisfying you by delivering your divine mission to every conditioned soul. Without this mission we would only die. During your presence we may have failed to appreciate who you were. Now we must glorify you by preaching Krsna consciousness in every town and village. You yourself have said that our love for you will be exhibited if we cooperate together to maintain and expand what you have already given us.

Srila Prabhupada, may our attachment for you always grow. In your concluding pastimes in Vrndavana, you were acting in so many ways, just so we would become attached to you. That is our perfection. Our duty as your disciples is to develop a feeling of separation from you. You have gone, and the separation is heartbreaking, but by your inconceivable mercy your presence is here even more. This is our only hope in life. Please allow us to develop this feeling for you. Please allow us to develop a remembrance of your instructions.

*prasangam ajaram pasam atmanah kavayo viduh*  
*sa eva sadhusu krto moksa-dvaram apavrtam*

**"Every learned man knows very well that attachment for the material is the greatest entanglement of the spirit soul. But that same attachment, when applied to self-realized devotees, opens the door of liberation." (Bhag. 3.25.20)**

Please let us transfer our desires to your lotus feet. Please give us your association.

*tulayama lavenapi na svargam napunarbhavam*  
*bhagavat-sangi-sangasya martyanam kim utasisah*

**"The value of a moment's association with the devotee of the Lord cannot even be compared to the attainment of heavenly planets or liberation from matter, and what to speak of worldly benedictions in the form of material prosperity, which is for those who are meant for death."**

Srila Prabhupada, your association is always desirable, it is worship-able, it is praiseworthy, and it is the highest goal of life. In your absence, please bless us with your association by awarding us engagement in your service and remembrance of your lotus feet.

## Venezuela farm

*nama om visnu-padaya krsna-presthaya bhu-tale*  
*srimate bhaktivedanta-svamin iti namine*

*namas te sarasvate deve gaura-vani-pracarine*  
*nirvisesa-sunyavadi-pascatya-desa-tarine*

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada, who is very dear to Lord Krsna, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism."**

All glories to you, O spiritual master. As Srila Visvanatha Cakravarti Thakura has said:

*samsara-davanala-lidha-loka-tranaya karunya-ghanaghanatvam*  
*praptasya kalyana-gunarnavasya vande guroh sri-caranaravindam*

**"The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a cloud pours water on a forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master delivers the materially afflicted world by extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is an ocean of auspicious qualities."**

Dear Gurudeva, by your mercy we can now understand that everybody on this planet is enamored by the illusory satisfaction derived from sense gratification. Being thus fully engaged in activities of illusion, they reject the Krsna consciousness movement, not seeing the benefit of surrendering to Krsna, and therefore they're subjected to different and variegated miserable conditions of life, foolishly attached to this material existence and to the so-called society, friendship and love. Everyone, man or woman, is attached to this material existence and is engaged, therefore, in cheating all others in a big society of cheaters and cheated. Thus, in this modern society people don't feel the need of inquiring about spiritual realization, and due to this they are full of anxieties and are strongly bound by material desires, keeping themselves in madness. So it is very difficult to get out of the material entanglement, but by one way or another we have been favored by Krsna. By His mercy we have been given the opportunity of associating with a pure devotee like Your Divine Grace.

We can thus conclude that we have been blessed by Krsna, for now we are becoming Krsna conscious and are engaged in the preaching work of this Vaisnava philosophy.

*Your most fallen servants,*  
New Bharatavarsa Venezuela Farm Project

## Vrndavana Dhama

Respected and honorable Vaisnavas of the world,

May I bring your kind attention to the divine resting place of our beloved Guru Maharaja, Sri Srimad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada. Before the holy *mandira* of Sri Sri Krsna-Balarama in the transcendental land of Vrndavana is situated the eternal form of our most beloved Gurudeva in the mood of *samadhi* upon Their Lordships.

May I humbly request that you once please come and throw yourself at the lotus feet of Srila Prabhupada, who has given the world the treasure of Krsna consciousness. And remain there in the dust of Vrndavana to hear Sri Krsna's names sung by Prabhupada's devotees by the *samadhi,* see the form of Srila Prabhupada everywhere, and touch the lovely land where Srila Prabhupada has lived. Every moment will be full with the bitter-sweet remembrance of Srila Prabhupada here in his external home. Absorbed in the pastimes of Prabhupada, remain fixed in Vrndavana at his lotus feet wherever you go.

Srila Prabhupada is here with his devotees, engaged in his eternal pastimes with Sri Sri Krsna and Balarama. Srila Prabhupada has not left us; nor have Krsna and Balarama left, though the historians may declare differently. Srila Prabhupada's home is here, and his pastimes continue eternally. Please remember the fantastic accounts of Sri Caitanya Maha-prabhu, who would come personally to the homes of His devotees and accept their offerings although physically He was not present. Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu appeared as the exemplary devotee of the Lord. Similarly, Srila Prabhupada appeared as the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead.

As the sun may appear for some time and then be covered by clouds, so Srila Prabhupada is simply beyond our vision. But his influence is experienced throughout the universe as the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. The shadow of separation is producing a darkness in our lives that only the remembrance of Srila Prabhupada can dispel.

The Supreme Lord Himself has made Srila Prabhupada the most memorable and famous person in the world. The greatness that Srila Prabhupada manifested while on this earth was just a spark of his splendor. Even in Krsna's personal *dhama,* Srila Prabhupada is considered the most famous person in the world. The devotees have constructed a gate at the entrance of Vrndavana that towers over Vrndavana's main road. The gate is called "Sri Bhaktivedanta Swami Dvara." The name of the main road is "Sri Bhaktivedanta Swami Marga." The most famous temple is Prabhupada's temple, Sri Krsna-Balarama Mandira, and the finest school is Sri Bhaktivedanta Swami Gurukula. The finest *gosala* is Bhaktivedanta Swami Gosala, and the finest publications of Vedic philosophy are produced by the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust. Everything about Srila Prabhupada is gloriously memorable and fantastic.

I offer my most humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master. His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupada, who is the greatest person in all of the spiritual and material worlds. I only pray to follow him lifetime after lifetime and remain his menial servant eternally.

*Disciples, Gurukula students and Children from*

Sri Krsna-Balarama Mandira and Sri Bhaktivedanta Swami Gurukula.

## Washington D.C.

Jaya Prabhupada !

O saintly sage,  
of this age of iron and irritation  
the news of your departure  
fell upon us  
like an avalanche of ice.  
The razors of separation  
began to cut...... and they will say  
you have gone away  
but still you dance  
through this cancerous world  
singing of "another nature. "  
Still you will give eternal shelter  
to the pathetic creatures of time.  
Still you will enter hollow hearts  
and make them full again.  
Still you will direct  
the spiritual derelicts  
across the strange  
seas of samsara.  
Still you will irrigate  
this arid world  
with your ceaseless streams  
of compassion.  
Still you will. . . because you are  
Srila Prabhupada,  
our matrix of realization,  
the destroyer of infatuation,  
an absolute personality,  
above maya's duality!

When they carried your holy body  
through the streets of Vrndavana  
thousands fell into the dust  
weeping and chanting  
Jaya Prabhupada! Jaya Prabhupada!'

O sinless swanlike soul  
now you have taken flight...  
... and behind you is a trail  
of literary light,  
a safe-passage  
through the vicious material night,  
a bridge back home  
to Govinda's realms of endless delight.  
Jaya Prabhupada! Jaya Prabhupada!  
Jaya Prabhupada!

New Hastinapura, Washington, D.C.

## West Africa

Dear Srila Prabhupada,

We have neither the spiritual maturity nor the devotional inclination to enable us to fully realize, appreciate and relish the enormous transcendental achievement which you have so adroitly and audaciously accomplished against all odds and expectations. Actually, our adulation of you is so imperfect that we can never dare to hope that it is even remotely acceptable to your completely spiritualized lotus feet. Nonetheless, we humbly submit this written homage to you, flawed as it is with our material conceptions, in the hope that we ourselves will become purified and enlightened by this attempt to glorify the most inconceivably glorious personality of this or any other age. Srila Prabhupada, we thank you for having come here and moreover for the wonderful magnanimity which you have displayed by accepting us as your servants. Our only misfortune is that your magnificent form is no longer manifest before us. Still, it somehow seems to us that you have not entirely left us, and we take comfort in the following verse:

He lives forever by his divine instructions and the follower lives with him.

Srila Prabhupada, all glories to you!

Your servants at ISKCON West Africa.

Jalakara dasa brahmacari */* Syamalal dasa brahmacari

## West African TSKP

Dear Srila Prabhupada,

Srila Bhaktisiddhanta Sarasvati Maharaja gave you the order to take this supreme science to the English-speaking countries of the West, and you expanded on the instructions to his ecstasy. On your order we are endeavoring to make Africa a "Black Vrndavana."

Every town and village here means a lifetime of engagement for this continent, so you have so mercifully guaranteed us full service for our idle minds and bodies. We, the above-mentioned. *sankirtana* party, are engaged in traveling sixty-five hundred miles throughout nine countries that have never seen a devotee of the Lord and certainly haven't rendered devotional service for quite some time. Owing to various political situations, we are experiencing great difficulty in crossing borders, and particularly into Africa itself. Krsna is eternal time, so He knows, but He is also the ability in man, so please, Srila Prabhupada, extract that ability from us. There are over fifty countries that we want to visit here in the next five years and try and plant a few seeds in each so we can select the very best countries and work on them.

You have said that your Guru Maharaja has arranged for so many of us, your disciples, to take birth in the West to help you carry on this mission of Caitanya Mahaprabhu; so similarly, Prabhupada, if you could arrange for some pious living entities to take birth in the right place in Africa, and then you guide us so we can open up Gurukula training centers in Africa, then local men can become Krsna conscious leaders. You have mentioned many times also that there is enough land to feed ten times more people on this planet, and Africa in particular has that potential. If we can set up model *varnasrama* farm communities, then the local people will quickly learn from the Vedic example, and then we feel we can offer whole nations to your lotus feet.

At this time we are engaged in the adventurous task of finding out by personal experience just how much we can do in each and every place, or if we can do anything at all. Please have a word in Radharani's ear, so She can use Her powerful influence on Sri Krsna, and if He wants then He will provide the *laksmi* and particularly the manpower for such an undertaking. All glories to you, Srila Prabhupada,

Your not-so-humble servants of the Great West African TSKP

Brahmananda Svami */* Anuttama dasa brahmacari */* Svarupananda dasa brahmacari */* Amita dasa brahmacari */* Hunkara dasa brahmacari */* Nayanabhirama dasa adhikari */* Mrgapati dasa brahmacari */* Ekadasidasa brahmacari
